Chapter 2951: Had To Run!

“Impressive. The logic of such an attack is completely different from common training techniques. I’ve even analyzed a lot of formulas about the energy transfer and utilization of the four-dimensional space. If it were a real-world battle, perhaps the Fist King’s attack would have opened multiple

wormholes on Lu Qingchen’s body and led him into the storm of the four-dimensional space, thereby destroying him completely!”

Li Yao exclaimed.

“It’s much more complicated than that. The boxing champion must’ve noticed that there was a serious incompatibility between Lu Qingchen and the ‘Fuxi data’ that he just absorbed. That’s why he introduced a weird power into the attack pattern from the magical universe on purpose to exacerbate the

‘incompatibility’ inside Lu Qingchen’s body and make his opponent self-contradictory. There was no need for him to attack at all. He was the one who broke down!”

The mental devil commented on them.

“Strong. He’s absolutely strong. As expected of the legendary ‘Punching King’!”

Li Yao was almost dancing and cheering for the boxing champion.

“Of course. That’s why I knew a long time ago that you were unreliable. At the crucial moment, you still needed the boxing champion to take action. You just had to watch from the auditorium and play the role of a cheerleader seriously!”

The mental devil hit the nail on the head.

While they were talking, they grinned hideously as if their scheme had worked out.

Lu Qingchen’s head was dizzy and he was vomiting blood because of the continuous explosions inside his body. He felt that the enormous database and information stream that he had just absorbed were going to be out of his control at any moment.

When he heard that Li Yao and the mental devil were echoing each other, he was more than infuriated, but there was nothing he could do about them.

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

‘As more and more rune arrays popped up, Lu Qingchen’s giant of light was blown beyond recognition and almost turned into a broken octopus. Shaken, he could only release chaotic data and information streams from his abdomen, which were the dead bodies of the ‘Fuxi’ that he had just swallowed.

This time, Lu Qingchen was really considering retreating.

He truly deserved to be one of the few heroes of the new generation in the entire Pangu Universe. He was well aware of the principle of making decisions when necessary. Even though he had gone through all the trouble and risked his life to absorb alll the data of the Fuxis, he did not hesitate at all when it

came to the point where he had to give them up. Instead, he treated a lot of ‘Fuxis’ data’ as a weapon and sprayed it at the Fist King, Li Yao, the mental devil, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, as if he were a thief who had spat out a lot of black ink before he ran away.

Although Fuxi had passed away, the data streams that he had accumulated for hundreds of thousands of years still had the supreme power of a flood or a ferocious beast. Li Yao and the others were covered in ‘ink, as if they had fallen into countless broken virtual mazes and were immersed in the broken

and twisted history.

In the meantime, the entire virtual space was shaking and collapsing.

The virtual space had been the core of the mind of the Fuxis in the first place. After the Fuxis were swallowed by Lu Qingchen, they naturally became Lu Qingchen’s nest. Right now, Lu Qingchen felt cold all over his body because of the killing of the Fist King. He wanted to run away, but he was no longer

able to maintain the stability of the virtual space. The entire space suddenly turned illusionary, pale, and dry. A large area of darkness fell off, revealing the mottled nature of the data. The collisions between the data and the formulas were more and more obvious, and there were more and more errors.

The entire space was burning furiously. Butterflies whose data had turned into ash were dancing and shrieking weirdly.

“Not good. This place is about to be destroyed. Run!”

Li Yao and the mental devil had never experienced such a weird situation before, either. Strictly speaking, they did not have shells right now. They were merely ghosts that were wandering between the crystal processor and the virtual world. They did not know what would happen to them after the virtual

space collapsed, either. However, they felt that it was not a good thing.

They had to find a data interface and escape before the virtual space collapsed completely!

But Lu Qingchen was faster than them. After more than half of the ‘Fuxi data’ was released, he finally regained absolute control over his soul. The giant of light blossomed like a lotus and split into six streaks of brightness that dashed out through the six data interfaces!

In the real world, the fierce war was still raging around the five supreme fortresses and Ultimate Rescue, which were the cores of the Fuxi fleet.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Although most of the starships of the fleet had been frozen in a weird way and did not know how to counterattack except to raise the highest level of spiritual shield and wait to be beaten, the five supreme fortresses each had a Grand Master who could fight independently and minimize the negative

effects of the ‘Fuxi collapse’.

Although the attackers were coming at them aggressively, they were too exhausted to blow up the five super star fortresses that were as magnificent as mountains because of the shortage of ammunition and the fatigue of the soldiers.

Therefore, the two parties were in an awkward stalemate for now, like two exhausted animals that had been fighting for three days and three nights. They glared at each other and breathed heavily.

Of course, compared to the coalition army of the federation and the imperium, which had lost all communication with ‘Ultimate Rescue’, the fleet of the Fuxis, which had no leader, was in a much worse state. In the cockpit of the five supreme fortresses, the five great masters were all spinning and

panicking like bees that had been seriously affected by a magnetic field, not knowing what to do.

‘There were indeed people among the five masters of kindness. Of course, they were not the ‘messengers of God’, but the toughest and most loyal puppets that had been carefully refined by the Fuxi. Their brains had been carefully modified, too. Theoretically speaking, they should maintain the highest

connection with the mainframe of ‘Ultimate Rescue’.

In other words, the mysterious data and information streams from ‘Ultimate Rescue’ were the easiest to hack and control the brains of the fleet of the Fuxis.

“God Fuxi, what happened to you? Did you hear the voice of your most loyal servant?”

At this moment, the five masters of kindness were on the bridge, their brains connected to dozens of crystal cables. They were begging for their god’s response and their direction of victory sincerely.

After calling for a long time, the direction of Ultimate Rescue’ was empty. It was like a silent abyss without any sound.

Although the five super experts had been deeply brainwashed and were extremely loyal, they could not help but feel somewhat frustrated and shaken when faced with the silent mainframe and the crazy enemy fire.

Right then—

Cracking noises were echoing inside the mainframe crystal processors of the five supreme fortresses. Gold streaks surged out from the depths of the crystal processors and flooded into the brains of the five masters through the crystal cables.

“God Fuxi?”

The five masters were overjoyed. “Is that you? You’ve finally heard the call of your most loyal servant!”

“Yes, Iam!

‘An anxious voice came from the depths of their brains. “Now, retreat. Retreat. Retreat at any cost. We don’t need too many soldiers for what we are going to do next. Quality over quantity. All we need to do is evacuate the five supreme fortresses!”

“But…”

The slightly weird voice and the way of communication startled the five masters of the Ultimate Kindness at the same time. They asked in confusion, “God Fuxi, why is your voice like this? And your scent. Also—”

Before they realized what was going on, the five streaks of golden brightness exploded into thousands of gold threads and pierced through almost every brain cell of the five masters.

“Cut the crap. This is the new me. Now, carry out the command!”

The voice roared.

After a violent shiver, bright gold blood was flowing out of their eyes, nostrils, ears, and mouth.

‘Their eyes were shattered and condensed again in the blink of an eye. They were completely different from before. They were more mechanical, but they were also more fanatical.

“Yes, God Fuxi. Your will is our life!”

The five sages were resolute.

In the meantime, deep inside ‘Ultimate Rescue’, inside the workshop of the Giant God Soldier.

The magnificent gold-bearded soldier suddenly broke free from all the restraints and the fuel-filled tubes and sat straight.

Shua!

On the head of the Giant God Soldier, the ring-shaped crystal cameras that constituted a band of brightness suddenly glittered, revealing Lu Qingchen’s furious and unwilling eyes.

Li Yao had once wondered why the Giant God Soldier did not have a ‘spiritual residence’, or in other words, a cockpit, and how it could be steered. Was it remotely controlled?

‘The answer was simple. The Giant God Soldier was not prepared for human beings at all. It had been modified beyond recognition and broken the restriction of ‘non-Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators are not allowed to control the Giant God Soldier’. It was an iron body specially prepared for the Fuxi Clan!

‘When necessary, Fuxi could ‘transfer’ his main database to the giant soldier and turn it into his shell to suppress the real world.

It was a pity that it failed to anticipate Li Yao’s sleazyness and Lu Qingchen’s forbearance. In the end, in its home field, under the joint attack of the two human beings, even the well-prepared giant soldier turned into Lu Qingchen’s trophies.

Crack! Crack!

Aseries of dazzling data flooded out of the mainframe crystal processor of the Giant God Soldier, allowing Lu Qingchen to fully understand the performance of the ‘Descender’.

CRACK! CRACK! CRACK!

The right arm of the ‘descender’ suddenly split, changed, and reassembled into a super crystal cannon that was revolving at a high speed. Electric arcs that looked like dragons were raging among the steel and the crystals, ready to attack.

Lu Qingchen aimed at the cockpit.

With one shot, he would be able to pierce through the entire ‘Ultimate Rescue’ and blow the cockpit into smithereens. At the same time, the array-like super computation system made of thousands of primeval crystal processors would be blown to pieces, accelerating the collapse of the virtual space..

Chapter 2952: Leave Me Alone, Just Go!

But right then, he hesitated. Complicated emotions that belonged to human beings were clearly revealed on the iron mask.

A series of information and data, including the fuel and ammunition of the ‘Descender’, as well as the situation of the battlefield in the periphery, including the battle parameters of the federal army and the imperial

army, the distribution of starships, and so on, popped up in Lu Qingchen’s head.

“I—I should bombard the cockroach without caring about anything else. The entire universe would be peaceful then!”

“However, I am not the natural master of the ‘Descender’. The Giant God Soldier has not fully accepted me yet. If I launch such an intense attack recklessly, it will be a great loss of fuel and ammunition. I’m afraid that it

will be unfavorable for my retreat later.

“Also, ‘Burning Prairie! and ‘Kunlun’, the main warships of the federation, are the closest to Ultimate Rescue’. It is estimated that, after the five supreme fortresses retreat from the battlefield, they will fill in the gaps

quickly and be the first to reach ‘Ultimate Rescue’, board the starship, and seize all the super crystal processors.

“The invaluable super crystal processors passed on from the primeval era could’ve been trophies of the Star Glory Federation and increased the capital for negotiations with the imperium. How could I destroy them so

easily?

“Well, then, then…”

Lu Qingchen hesitated for a long time. The muzzle of the gun was blinking and shivering.

After hesitating for a long time, he gritted his teeth and extinguished the flames on the muzzle, turning the super crystal cannon back into a cold arm.

“Forget it. I’ll let you go for now and let you be happy for another two days.

“Maybe such a ‘splendid victory’ is more beneficial for a cockroach such as yourself to numb your nerves and conceal my real purpose.

“When I find ‘that place’ and swallow another Fuxi and all the things that it protects, I will come back and settle things with a cockroach like you!”

Thinking about that, Lu Qingchen suddenly rose up. The ‘Descender’ of the Giant God Soldier soared into the sky and burnt a transparent hole in the middle of ‘Ultimate Rescue’. He roared and charged into the depths

of the sea of stars!

“The enemy is running away!”

n the depths of the sea of stars, Ding Lingdang, who was riding the broken ‘Great Fire Dragon Sparrow’, was the first to sense the anomaly of the fleet of the Fuxi Clan.

As an invisible ripple spread out from ‘Ultimate Rescue’ to the five supreme fortresses, and then from the five supreme fortresses to the entire fleet, the fleet of the Fuxi Clan was finally freed from the stunned state and

regained part of its vitality.

However, their battle formation had been cut into pieces by the two scalpels of the federal army and the imperial army. Almost all the starships had been surrounded by more than twice the number of enemy starships.

They were caught in a dilemma of fighting alone. It was impossible for the gods to form an effective counterattack even if they were the commanders.

The members of the Covenant Alliance realized the same thing and stopped trying to take over the capital city. Instead, they refilled their spiritual shields and power units with all the crystal fuel they had. A large

batch of starships crowded forward as human shields to attract the firepower of the federal army and the imperial army. They covered the five supreme fortresses and gradually retreated from the battlefield.

it was true that, as long as a starship was engaged with the enemy’s firepower and left cracks on the shell, it would be very difficult to enter the four-dimensional space jump state.

However, the five supreme fortresses were all super-large fortresses that were as large as asteroids. Not only were their composite armor unbelievably thick, even their spiritual shields were as thick as a real city wall.

The federal army and the imperial army, which were running out of supplies and strength, would not be able to breach through the solid defense of the enemy for a long time.

Thousands of dazzling flames were spurting out of the five supreme fortresses. They gradually accelerated to the limit of regular sailing and fled toward the periphery of the Empyrean Terminus Sector like five

rampaging asteroids.

In the meantime, more and more starships of the Covenant Alliance were launching suicidal attacks at the federal army and the imperium, not to destroy the enemy but to blow themselves up into stone belts to block

the enemy’s pursuit.

Ding Lingdang was burning with anxiety. She gritted her teeth and cut apart the debris one piece after another, piercing through one starship after another. But looking at the fuel and ammunition that she was

consuming, she still felt helpless.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

It could not be helped. The difference in strength between the two sides was not large enough for the coalition army of the federation and the imperium to completely annihilate the fleet of the Fuxi fleet.

Or rather, at the beginning of the war, their sole purpose had been to defend the Empyreal Terminus Sector with all their might. They had even been prepared to fight in the alleys of the planet. Nobody had expected

that the war would be reversed and that they would be the winners of the war. Nobody had been prepared to pursue the enemy.

The main brain of the fleet of the Fuxi fleet had been destroyed. But right now, a small head that was as brutal and resolute as the main brain of the fleet had grown out. Nobody would be able to stop Lu Qingchen and

the five supreme fortresses from running away.

nowing that, Ding Lingdang was unwilling to give up. She was planning to chase after the five supreme fortresses for another hundred thousand kilometers, when a streak of gold suddenly soared into the sky and

lunged at her.

BOOM! BOOM BOOM BOOM BOOM!

n the blink of an eye, the two parties had exchanged over a hundred blows. All the debris within a radius of three to five kilometers was affected by the spiritual flames and turned into burning powder.

“So strong!”

Ding Lingdang’s pupils constricted violently, but the pores all over her body all opened up and spurted out crimson spiritual gas, ready to fight.

The members of the Covenant Alliance were not best known for their individual combat ability. Even though Ding Lingdang had encountered a few Giant God Soldiers of the Covenant Alliance just now, it was not very challenging for

her.

However, the giant gold-bearded soldier gave her a completely different feeling. It was an opponent that was truly worth a fight.

The only drawback was that her fuel and ammunition index were both blinking in red. The overall structure of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow had reached the limit of collapse, too. There was no need for the enemy to

be heavy-handed at all. Even if she unleashed her strongest spiritual flames, it was possible that the Giant God Soldier would be blown apart on the spot.

Besides, although she was surrounded by spluttering electric arcs, there was no intense killing intent, as if she was unwilling to be her enemy.

“Stop chasing. It’s useless. It’s just a waste of fuel. You can’t stop me from retreating.”

A weird ripple suddenly spread out from the golden-bearded giant soldier. It turned into a low and indifferent voice inside the mainframe crystal processor of the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow. The voice was

intentionally suppressed, not willing to let Ding Lingdang recognize its true identity at this moment. “You are the Speaker of Parliament of the federation. I don’t want to kill you, so don’t push me too far. Remember,

your real enemy is not me, but the imperium behind you!

“There’s no way that the five supreme fortresses will be left to you. But Ultimate Rescue’, the flagship of the fleet of the Fuxis, is right there. The trophies on it are precious for the entire federation. Also… whoever you

want to find, don’t waste your time on me. Just do what the Speaker of the Federation should do!

Hardly had the gold-bearded soldier finished his sentence when he gradually vanished from Ding Lingdang’s sight at a lightning fast speed.

“This is…”

Ding Lingdang stared blankly at the precise coordinates sent by the other party.

She had a vague feeling that she had heard the other party’s voice somewhere before. It was even very familiar. She did not even need to analyze it. Her intuition told her that there was nothing wrong with the

coordinates.

But how was it possible? Who was he exactly?

Ding Lingdang, as well as all the soldiers of the federal army and the imperial army, were gazing at the empty, starry universe at the edge of the Empyrean Terminus Sector.

n that place, the surging tides raised by the five supreme fortresses tearing apart the three-dimensional space gradually dispersed in a form as gentle and splendid as an aurora, as if a veil had been draped over the

brutal battlefield. It was a declaration that the battle of the capital city was coming to an end in an unexpected way.

As the five supreme fortresses teleported into the four-dimensional space and vanished into the depths of the sea of stars, the fleet of the Fuxi Clan lost their main brain again and was completely lost and paralyzed.

The coalition army of the federation and the imperium had almost emptied the last magazine, the last barrel, and the last bit of fuel.

Even many soldiers who had been fighting for ten days and ten nights fell to the ground with earsplitting snores after their last nerve was broken.

“Is this… the end?”

Inside the cockpit of Prairie Fire, Bai Kaixin observed the bouncing waves of the five supreme fortresses in the distance and exclaimed in fear.

“At least for now, right?”

On the alloy virus that had been connected to the same tactical command system as Prairie Fire, Boss Bai took a long breath in relief and sat down on the command seat. A smile of relief appeared on his face, but he

could not help but tilt his head to the side.

… The Jade Cauldron Sector, the Yellow Dragon Sector, and the Empyreal Terminus Sector had been engaged in a series of fierce battles. Every battle was perilous, and every battle had been reversed multiple times.

Even the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators were exhausted and could not hold on any longer!

“No. It’s not over yet.”

Inside the Great Fire Dragon Sparrow, Ding Lingdang narrowed her eyes and gazed at the direction of Ultimate Rescue. “At least there, it is not over yet.”

“AHHHHHHHHH!”

In the virtual space that was about to collapse, the ocean of data turned into a destructive swirl. The 50% of the data that Lu Qingchen spurted out was like a ferocious beast in the swirl. With the instincts that

corrupted everything, it chased after Li Yao, the mental devil, the Fist King, Little Ming, and Wenwen, making everyone except the Fist King scream miserably.

“This way. It is the data channel that I created when I forced my way in!”

The Fist King waved his iron arm. The tip of his fist actually blasted a golden path above the virtual space.

“Not good. We are tied up by the data turbulences that have completely gone out of control. It will be difficult to get rid of them!”

Xiao Ming and Wen Wen cried in unison.

“You go first. I’ll deal with them!”

Li Yao gritted his teeth and threw the two kids to the ground. Then he turned around and dashed toward the data turbulence.

“Dad!”

Xiao Ming exclaimed.

“Dad!”

Wen Wen screamed.

“Li Yao!”

Even the boxing champion was somewhat moved.

“Leave me alone. Go now. Go now!”

Li Yao roared.

“I really want to go. Just let me go and stop bothering me!”

Looking at his tail that had been tangled by Li Yao, the mental devil was about to cry. “What’s the meaning of this? Are you trying to drag me down with you? What did I do to offend you? Can you spare my life?”

“No problem. Go now. I’ll take care of everything here. I’ll resist the damn ‘Fuxi Data Turbulence’.”

Li Yao looked at the mental devil sincerely, but he tightened his grip on its tail. “Leave me alone. Run away with Xiao Ming and Wenwen. Leave me alone!”.

Chapter 2953: Collapse And Reconstruction

The mental devil wanted to cry but had no tears. It tried its best to run away, only to be tied up by Li Yao and dragged back by Li Yao.

“Wow!”

Holding the mental devil, Li Yao said, his eyes brimming with tears, “I didn’t expect that you would be so loyal at the critical moment and willing to die with me. As the saying goes, a friend in need is a friend indeed. It

seems that I was wrong about you. I apologize. Good brother, let’s not talk about anything else. Today, we will live and die together!”

“…To hell with you!”

The mental devil was crying and screaming, twisting in vain. “What did I do wrong when I was a Blood Stripe Virus in my previous life to deserve such a ‘good brother’ like you? You can go to hell if you want to. I’m still

young. I still have my youth. I don’t want to die. I—I—if I had known this would happen, I would’ve joined hands with Lu Qingchen and killed—”

The mental devil’s noises came to an abrupt end.

The data turbulence that was as furious as a tornado and as chaotic as a swirl that could swallow the sky and the earth had sucked them in.

For a moment, Li Yao and the mental devil both felt that the world was spinning. In a trance, they experienced the changes of thousands of years. Their souls were mottled by the endless data, like statues that had been

eroded by the wind and were riddled with holes.

“Dad!”

“Li Yao!”

At the last moment, they heard a familiar voice above their heads, and a drop of vague brightness was growing larger and larger.

It was Xiao Ming, Wenwen, and the Fist King. Instead of leaving, they came to Li Yao’s rescue and faced the ‘Fuxi Data Turbulence’ at the risk of their lives being destroyed!

Li Yao was overjoyed. But when he thought of the possibility that Xiao Ming and Wen Wen might have been involved in the weird swirl, too, and that they might have been corrupted by the highly corrosive data

turbulences of the Fuxis, his soul tightened like a steel rope. A brand-new strength seemed to have grown out of nowhere. His earthworm-like body expanded continuously, until it was as large as a mountain. His

flagella grew again, turning into strong arms that supported the furious ocean of data like ancient gods.

“I will never let you corrupt Xiao Ming and Wen Wen!”

BOOM!

Li Yao felt that every piece of data inside the swirl had turned into a shining star, and 1.08 billion stars were exploding right in front of his face, blowing him into nothingness.

He had no idea what happened next.

He was sleeping, he was thinking, and he was reconstructing his soul in the reconstruction of the broken virtual world while he explored and released the strength in the deepest part of his soul.

Just like Lu Qingchen not long ago, he had experienced multiple reincarnations in the ‘mother body’. His consciousness had been divided into several levels from the inside to the outside. The antennae of his mind

sometimes extended to the other side of the vast sea of stars, and sometimes shrank to the streams of data that looked like plankton.

Sometimes, he was like an immortal single-celled creature who had gone through a long evolution in a virtual world that was neither real nor false. He turned from a single-celled to multiple-celled, from a fungus to a

trilobite, from a fish to an amphibian, from a dinosaur to an ape, and even into a group of beetles like the Houyi Clan or a giant tree like the Kuafu Clan.

Sometimes, his telepathic thoughts could flow out of ‘Ultimate Rescue’ through the open data interface. He could see clearly how the broken, twisted flagship of the Fuxi fleet was captured by other starships and

dragged between two magnificent warships. Although he did not recognize the models of the two warships, he was greatly relieved when he saw the glittering Rising Dragon of the Nine Stars emblem on the head of

the starship.

His telepathic thoughts continued extending. He seemed to see the coalition army of the federation and the imperium chasing after each other at the edge of the Empyreal Terminus Sector, sweeping away the

remaining forces of the Fuxi fleet, including the reconstruction of the imperial capital, the surrender of the Yellow Dragon Sector, and the chaos and collapse of the territories of the four electors’ families.

He was not sure whether it was real or just his imagination.

Just as he wasn’t sure whether or not the illusionary and real worlds that he had experienced were real, primeval, ancient, ancient, medieval, and vintage world of sword immortals, the Great Dark Age ruled by the

demon race, and the endless glory of the Star Ocean Imperium…

Then again, how should he distinguish the truth from the false? He remembered that he had argued with Fuxi and Lu Qingchen about the question. Was the world created by data and information more false than the

world built by Cultivators for forty thousand years and therefore less valuable? How could he be sure that the world built by Cultivators for forty thousand years, including himself, was real?

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Maybe, every intelligent creature was ‘false’ to some extent when they were just born. That was why they had to spend their entire life on ‘cultivation’!

While he was thinking, he was enlightened from the deepest part of his soul. After a ‘ding’ sound, the shell of a certain crystal cracked, and something as pure and weak as a newborn baby that was full of possibilities

broke out of the cocoon.

He, Li Yao, had finally woken up again after a long sleep, absorption, integration, and training!

“This is…”

Li Yao felt that he was a fetus in amniotic fluid. There was an indescribable tranquility and tranquility. He saw that he and the mental devil had melded into one again like a giant gene strand. The upper half of his body

turned into a human being again, except that it was translucent and overflowing with brilliance. Every pore on his body was flowing with brilliance that seemed to be real.

He raised his hand and analyzed all the data that constituted the body. To his surprise, Li Yao discovered that his soul had been embedded into part of the database of the Fuxis perfectly, allowing him to grasp part of

the memories and abilities of the Fuxis. Of course, he also gained part of the permissions of ‘Ultimate Rescue’ and the thousands of primeval crystal processors.

However, the data pieces of the Fuxis were too few after all. They were not enough to give him an overwhelming advantage. Therefore, they were suppressed by him and became part of him.

This was still a virtual space.

tt was not hard to tell that the virtual space was on the verge of collapse after the fierce battle a long time ago. But somehow, it managed to stay on the verge of collapse and was rescued again.

At this moment, countless virtual worlds, which were full of bumps and hollows, were soaking in the ocean of data that had calmed down and recovering their vitality like traumatized cells.

As for the virtual figures in the virtual world, it was impossible for them to realize what was going on in the outside world. The only thing they experienced was the apocalypse-like ‘calamity’ that seemed to be the end

of the world.

Some of the virtual worlds had completely collapsed. The data had turned into lifeless gray and white. There were no changes at all.

However, in more virtual worlds, after the tenacious people were dragged back to the starting point of evolution by the catastrophe, they started a new cycle of struggling for survival.

Li Yao was greatly touched by the lively scenes of reconstruction. For a moment, he really didn’t know how to distinguish the virtual figures made of data from real lives.

In the legends, human beings were made of mud by the Nuwa Clan.

Therefore, the kids made of mud were not qualified to determine who was alive and who was not.

Then, Li Yao saw translucent threads of brightness extending from thousands of virtual worlds that were being reconstructed and connecting to him.

He had a weird feeling that he was absorbing nutrition from the virtual worlds nonstop.

The more tenacious and evolved the creatures in the virtual world were, the more likely the data would change and the more nutrition he could collect from the virtual world.

It was with the nourishment of countless virtual worlds that he was able to recover from the withering state so quickly. He was able to vaguely pry into an unprecedented, indescribable, and undefined new level!

“Dad!”

While Li Yao was pondering the connection between himself and the thousands of virtual worlds, including the entire virtual space, he suddenly heard a crisp shout.

It sounded like Xiao Ming’s voice, but there was something wrong with it. The voice was too coarse. It did not sound like the voice of a seven-year-old, but more like that of a teenager who was in the voice-changing

stage.

Then, a tall, handsome young man swam out of the ocean of data and looked at him with a smile. “Dad!”

Li Yao was dumbfounded.

The young man looked almost the same as when he was in the magical equipment graveyard. He was equally handsome, unrestrained, and full of wildness.

The problem was, he did not have such a big son!

“Xiao Ming?”

Sensing the familiar scent from the stranger, Li Yao asked in disbelief, “How—how did you end up like this?”

“I’ve grown up. Every child has to grow up eventually, right?”

The black-haired teenager, who had a wild and uninhibited temperament, laughed heartily. He stretched his lively limbs towards Li Yao and flaunted the brand-new, glittering database to his heart’s content. “Or, to put

it another way, I have absorbed a large amount of data left behind by the Fuxi. I have analyzed and grasped countless algorithms from the primeval era as well as the logical thinking architecture of the Fuxi. With this as

the foundation, I have achieved a brand-new upgrade, and I have advanced to the next version. Otherwise, it would have been impossible for me to rebuild the collapsed virtual space and find a way to reconstruct my

father’s fragmented soul!”

Chapter 2954: Master Of Fuxi

“You swallowed the chaotic data pieces of the Fuxis?”

Li Yao was greatly alarmed.

The data pieces of the Fuxi Clan were not something that could be absorbed easily.

Although the core database of the Fuxis had been shattered and the underlying logic structure had collapsed, which equaled to the annihilation of their soul, they no longer had independent self-awareness.

However, the remaining data and information were too enormous. Some of the data fragments might still have commands that were similar to the instincts of ferocious beasts or even viruses.

The natural instincts of ferocious beasts were to attack and swallow, and the natural instincts of viruses were to corrupt and spread. It would be extremely troublesome if even a tiny bit of the virus touched one’s body.

it was like although Lu Qingchen had ‘killed’ the Fuxi and swallowed its ‘body’, he was still influenced by the natural instincts of the Fuxi and gradually turned into a second one.

Chapter 2957: Post-War Situation

“Wait, wait, wait. My head is in a mess right now. Things might not be as bad as they seem. In any case, I still choose to believe in the wisdom and kindness of human beings.

Li Yao thought carefully and said, “However, I can understand your, Wenwen’s, and the Fist King’s choice. Indeed, from your point of view, it is impossible for you to trust the human beings in the narrow sense,

especially the Immortal Cultivators such as the Imperium of True Human Beings.

“However, what are the reactions of the federation and the imperium? Are they going to stand by and watch you take over the fleet of the Fuxis? Where are we right now? I mean, where is Ultimate Rescue and the

entire fleet of the Fuxis?”

“Of course, the citizens of the federation and the imperium were greatly shocked by our sudden appearance. Although some of them, the participants of the ‘high-end forums of abnormal human beings’, knew of our

existence, most of them were not aware of it. When we took over the fleet of the Fuxi Clan, it was no longer a secret.

Xiao Ming said, “I have no idea what is on the mind of most human beings. I don’t think they will have a good impression of us. However, what can they do even if they are infuriated? The coalition army of the

federation and the imperium is already exhausted. If they continue fighting, they will only accelerate their self-destruction. They won’t be able to stop us at all. Besides, Wenwen and I control the entire battle network

of the imperial capital, and the Fist King has a very high clearance for the ‘Arsonist Fleet’. Of course, we don’t have the ability to defeat all our opponents, but it is more than enough for us to fight to the death.

“The triangle is the most stable structure. The Star Glory Federation alone is not enough to compete with the entire Imperium of True Human Beings. We sent a message to the federation as soon as possible. We are

willing to sign the most solid treaty with the Star Glory Federation on behalf of the Holy Accord Alliance and supervise the revolution of the imperium together with the federation. The federation is also welcome to

supervise and even participate in the treatment of the citizens of the Covenant Alliance.

“At that time, the federation could not find any reason to refuse.

“And once the Federation’s expedition army and the Fuxi Fleet gradually form an alliance, it will be impossible for the citizens of the Star Ocean Imperium to annihilate us.

“On the contrary, if the revolutionaries want to take control of the center of the sea of stars, eliminate the remaining forces of the four electors as quickly as possible, restore order in the sea of stars, and avoid a repeat

of the past, the crystal processor and the information technology of our society will have to be further improved. It is impossible to eliminate the factors that affect the life of information.

“Our young emperor uncle is a smart man who can see the big picture. On a certain level, he is even smarter than our father and more suitable to be an excellent ruler. Besides, we’ve been together for a long time. He

has a deep understanding about us and does not have the concerns and biases that ordinary people have about the life of information. Therefore, he didn’t do anything stupid and chose to accept us and provide the

most basic resources and facilities for us.

“We are now on the most remote and desolate abandoned asteroid base in the Empyrean Terminus Sector.

“Although resources are scarce here, and we can’t even see a star, the facilities built hundreds of years ago are all intact and ready for use after simple maintenance. With the resources provided by the young emperor

and the reserves of the fleet of the Fuxi Clan, we will be able to catch our breath for now.

While Xiao Ming was talking, dozens of 3D light beams were shining behind him, displaying the lively scenes of maintenance and construction of the abandoned base.

It could be clearly seen that, although many starships of the Fuxi fleet were riddled with holes, their plate armor battered, and they looked like ragged clothes, they managed to maintain the integrity of the basic

structure and the last bit of vitality of the Holy Accord Alliance as well as the first hope of the life of information.

“It took the entire capital city about a week to completely end the unrest and restore basic order.

Xiao Ming said, “After that, the fleet of the Fuxi fleet, the expedition army of the federation, and the leadership of the imperium of the young emperor’s uncle held a secret meeting of the highest level on Empyrean

Terminus. Everybody accepted the fact that there was nothing they could do about it, and the three parties were officially established.

“Although this is not the most ideal ending where ‘justice triumphs over evil and all problems cease to exist’ and where there are still very serious disagreements and suspicions between the two parties, and who knows

when there will be more trouble, at least temporary peace has arrived. Peace, even if it is only for one second, is better than nothing, isn’t it?”

Li Yao agreed.

But he did not see it coming. “So fast? You met me in secret while I was asleep?”

“Yes. Well, both the Covenant Alliance, the imperium, and everybody in the federation thought that it was better to meet while my father was away.

Xiao Ming said, “If so, the atmosphere of the meeting will be more solemn, and we will be able to discuss the issues of interest more intensely without any obstacles.”

“What do you mean?”

Li Yao was dazed for a moment, as if he had been wronged. “So, if I were there, the atmosphere of the meeting wouldn’t have been so solemn, would it? Who said that? I am the supreme ruler of the universe, the father

of the federation, the best expert in the Pangu Universe, the god slayer, the Black Wind King, and the high-dimensional virus. How am I not solemn? How am I ‘obstacles’2”

“,,.Don’t be excited, Dad. It’s not that we don’t like you. It’s just that your identity is too special, and you’ve made tremendous contributions to the civilization of mankind. Your contributions are unparalleled in the past

ten thousand years and the next ten thousand years.

Xiao Ming explained, “Look, you are the father of the Star Glory Federation and the husband of the incumbent Speaker of the Federation. In the meantime, you are the Black Wind King of the Imperium of True Human

Beings and one of the two most powerful warlords. You are also the foster brother of the emperor and one of the behind-the-scenes advocates who put the emperor on the supreme throne. You are also the one who

saved the capital city twice in only half a year. Wu Yingqi, the Black Star Emperor in the first time, and Fuxi in the second time. It is safe to say that you blew them up with your own hands. From that point of view, it is

not an exaggeration to call you the ‘first person to rebuild the imperium’. As for the Covenant Alliance, it is needless to say that your child has replaced the position of the Fuxi and is managing the entire Covenant Alliance

Alliance.

“Therefore, think about it. If you participate in such a high-level meeting, when the interests of the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance are in conflict, and everyone is fighting for their interests, which

side will you help?”

“Well…”

Li Yao thought for a long time and failed to come up with an answer. He could only mumble, “Alright. It seems that I’ve climbed too high without me knowing. My position is too high, and I’m no longer suitable for

games such as the Star Ocean Imperium. The national affairs such as the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance should be handled by you kids. Then, how are you doing?”

“So far, so good.”

Xiao Ming counted his fingers and introduced them to Li Yao one by one. “First of all, the fleet of the Fuxi fleet has completely given up attacking. They are crouching near the abandoned base at the edge of the

Empyrean Terminus Sector. The imperial army has deployed dense cosmic mines between this place and Empyrean Terminus to make sure that the fleet of the Fuxi fleet cannot pose any threat.

“Then, according to the conditions of the surrender, we disarmed about half of the starships, two thirds of which were handed over to the federation and one third to the imperium. We also provided the critical

parameters and structural designs of the starships of the Covenant Alliance to both sides.

n exchange, the Star Ocean Imperium and the federation accepted the surrender of the Star Glory Federation and offered part of the limited assets to us, ensuring the survival of most of the citizens of the Star Glory

ederation.

n the meantime, the Star Ocean Imperium and the federation also agreed that, as long as the humanitarian disaster inside the territory of the Star Glory Federation was not completely out of control, they would not

ttack the territory of the Star Glory Federation and maintain the existence of the Star Glory Federation as a political entity. They also reluctantly accepted the fact that the political entity would be managed by

rtificial intelligence for a long time to come.

n the meantime, the two countries will try their best to control the public opinion at home and guide the public’s opinion about the people of the Star Glory Federation and the artificial intelligence to meld with each

ther, or at least to understand each other.

don’t know how binding such a promise is, but that’s all for now.

“After the secret agreement was established on Empyrean Terminus and half of the armed fleet of the Fuxi fleet was disarmed and handed over, the rest and supplies of the coalition army of the federation and the

imperium were mostly completed, too. Therefore, their vanguard pointed at the territories of the four electors’ families and launched a new round of attacks on the Yellow Dragon Sector, the heart of the old imperium.

“The smoothness of the attack could not be described as ‘crushing’, but ‘passing on’. The worlds under the control of the four electors’ families, including those of the Yellow Dragon Sector, had been in a state of panic

for a long time. Their only hope was that the new imperium and the Holy Alliance would suffer heavy losses and they could fish in troubled waters for a while longer. Now that the imperial capital had been pacified, the

Holy Alliance had surrendered to the imperium, and they had a strong ally named the federation, their last hope was dashed.

“From below the Yellow Dragon Sector, the nobles of the other worlds were all fighting to surrender. Some of them were even stabbing each other in the back in order to make up for their mistakes with trophies. Most

of the time, the coalition army of the federation and the imperium did not even need to send out a single soldier. They could hear the roars of ‘Long live Emperor Yongjia’, one planet after another.

“That being said, the territories of the four electors’ families are too vast, and there are too many important facilities and space jump nodes that need to be received. Even if every Sector sends a fleet to defend them,

their military strength is far from enough. Therefore, almost all the leadership of the federation and the new imperium have gone out to supervise the surrender of the four electors and restore the local order as soon

as possible. It is also the reason why I am left here alone..

Chapter 2958: The Real ‘Holy Accord”!

“So it was like this!”

At first, Li Yao felt a bit jealous. He thought to himself that, with his identity and position right now, when he woke up, the Speaker of Parliament, the emperor, and the leader of the Holy Alliance should be cheering

beside his bed, right? It would be too lonely if he was alone.

However, on second thought, it did make sense. Although the Millennium War had come to an end, the aftermath that swept across the entire Pangu Universe would not die down so easily. There was still a long way to

go before the civilization of mankind was freed from the mud of suspicion and wariness and embarked on the golden road of mutual tolerance, understanding, and integration.

In the meantime, the citizens of the federation, the imperium, the Cultivators, the Immortal Cultivators, and even the citizens and the information lifeforms of the Star Glory Federation would have to tread on thin ice

and pay a hundred times more effort in order to find the precious hope.

Ding Lingdang and the rest of them must be fighting hard for the same purpose.

This was great!

“Right, where is Wen Wen?”

Li Yao thought for a moment. “Other people are too busy pacifying the remaining forces of the four electors to spare any time. Where is Wenwen? Why is she not looking for her father?”

“Wenwen went to the hinterland of the Covenant Alliance, where the Sectors were running dry and collapsing, to control the super crystal processors there and the ‘Ultimate Kindness’, who were responsible for the

maintenance of the super crystal processors.

Xiao Ming said, “Dad, you can imagine the situation of the Sectors right now, can’t you? They’ve almost used up their last crystal and their last bag of food. They’ve lost the control of the mainframe and even lost all

hope for the gods. What do you think will happen to the ‘soldier bees! and the ‘worker bees’? Maybe, they will turn into rusted machines and sit on the spot dumbly, enduring hunger and thirst until they fall to the

ground one by one and die in silence. Maybe, when their lives are seriously threatened, their survival instincts will be fully activated and evolve into the bestial instincts that feed on the flesh and blood of their own kind

so that they can live longer and turn their world into a primitive jungle and a living hell.

“Wenwen could not bear to see such a tragedy. Therefore, she took away about half of the super crystal processors on Ultimate Rescue and built a new… computational center that could be called the ‘main brain’ with

the algorithms left by the Fuxis. Then she went deep into the heartland of the Holy Alliance.

“To be honest, I tried to persuade her not to go. Such an approach would’ve burdened the entire Holy Alliance and tied her up. More than 95% of her computational ability would’ve been wasted, and she wouldn’t have

been able to think freely in the sea of stars like me anymore.

“Besides, by doing so, she might not be able to save all the people of the Star Glory Federation. On the contrary, human beings will be suspicious and think that she has the ambition to replace the Fuxi.

“The funny human beings, when they watch billions of people of the Star Glory Federation starve to death or die in the chaos of the collapse of order, will not be touched at all. They will only think that the people of the

Star Glory Federation brought it upon themselves. However, when they find that the people of the Star Glory Federation are under the control of ‘artificial intelligence’ again, it will be as if the ‘dignity of mankind’ has

been violated and desecrated in the worst way.

“Therefore, I repeatedly warned Wenwen not to do such a thankless task. Even if she really wanted to resolve the humanitarian disaster and show our kindness to mankind, there was no need for her to save all the

worlds of the Covenant Alliance at the same time. It was better for her to pick the industrial bases, the jump points, and the more valuable worlds.

“But she wouldn’t listen to me. She insisted that she would clean up the mess left by the Fuxis by herself and build a new Covenant Alliance. What could I do?

“The incident involved most of her computational ability. Besides, in the central area of the Holy Alliance, where resources were running dry, the supplies that she could get were limited, too. Over the past few days,

her communication with the Empyreal Terminus Sector had been intermittent. Unless it was absolutely necessary, she barely received any messages back, not to mention that her enormous database had been sent

back to this place to meet her father. It would consume too many resources, and Wenwen would certainly be reluctant to part with them.

“However, before she left, she left a letter for you. Here, this is it.”

A golden ball appeared on Xiao Ming’s fingertip, which was spluttering bright and firm sparks.

“Is—is that so?”

For a moment, Li Yao was in a trance. He didn’t know how to comment on the culture. Taking a deep breath, he extended one of his fingers and touched the gold ball on Xiao Ming’s fingertip, too.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

The golden ball suddenly shattered. The pieces turned into streaks of gold brightness and soared into the sky, interweaving and intertwining with each other. In the end, they condensed into the appearance of a

thirteen-year-old girl.

Of course, the girl did not look much like Li Yao.

However, she did not look like the valiant and passionate Ding Lingdang.

However, there was something vaguely resembling Li Yao’s old friend, Wei Qingqing.

It was Sister Qingqing, who had fought against the Ghost Face Silver Mosquitoes as a culture-type cultivator on the Grand Desolate War Institution’s train in the Heaven’s Origin Sector and sacrificed herself for a train

full of ordinary people. Later, she reincarnated as a spectral cultivator and eventually became the core of the “Tinder Plan”, jumping to the other side of the sea of stars.

However, on the basis of Wei Qingqing’s temperament, which was like an orchid in an empty valley, there was also a hint of compassion. There was even a hint of… divinity.

She was wearing a pure white gauze, with a few bracelets and anklets made of thorns on her slender hands and feet. Her long, black hair was flowing down like a waterfall, reflecting the rhythm of life all the time. Her

face was solemn and vintage, as if she had come from the origin of the civilization hundreds of thousands of years ago. If she had a willow branch in her hand, she would be able to create a brand-new creature easily.

“Wow!”

Li Yao was dumbfounded. He felt that he was about to be blinded by the brilliance of literature. The question mark of ‘What did I do to deserve such a daughter’ popped up in his mind.

“Dad, if you can read this letter, it means that your soul has been condensed and awakened from the abyss. How wonderful!”

There was still innocence in her gentle smile, but there was persistence and stubbornness in it. She said, “Maybe, when you wake up, you will find that everything is different from what you imagined. We did defeat

uxi, but we failed to annihilate him. We even had to accept him as part of us in order to continue the arduous journey of civilization.

Lu Qingchen’s ideas were too extreme, but it did not mean that everything he said was unreasonable. At least, he was right about one thing—the civilization of mankind needed a Fuxi.

“Yes. Not just the current Holy Accord Alliance, but also the Star Glory Federation and the Imperium of True Human Beings in the future. The entire civilization of mankind needs a Fuxi, a master of order and a

historian who has nothing to do with it, a clear-headed spectator, and a benevolent symbiont.

“It’s a simple logic.

“The civilization of mankind today has long broken away from the pastoral era of farming and weaving. It is accelerating toward the endless distance of information, virtualization, and networking. The civilization of

mankind today has spread to thousands of Sectors and tens of thousands of planets. The civilization of mankind in the future will march toward the vaster universe and the more unpredictable universe.

“However, it is impossible for human beings who evolved in the form of naked apes to adapt to such a bright and vast future on their own. Even if human beings entered the era of universal Cultivation and everybody

could reach the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage, it was still impossible for them to communicate with their compatriots millions of lightyears away on their own.

“The limits of information communication and resource delivery determine the limits of an imperium or even a civilization. The collapse of the Star Ocean Imperium, the Star Ocean Imperium, and the Imperium of

True Human Beings was precisely because they had crossed their own limits.

“If we want all mankind to be united, to understand and defend peace to the greatest extent, and to maintain the continuous development of civilization, the development of crystal processors and the Spiritual Nexus,

and even the birth and expansion of information life, it is inevitable and unavoidable.

“It is impossible for human beings to develop a super crystal processor whose computational ability exceeds the limits and send astronomical information into its brain for computation so that it can be extended to

every aspect of human society. It is also impossible for human beings to stop it from developing self-awareness and real wisdom and regard it as both a genius and an idiotic slave forever.

“If human beings’ attitude toward super crystal processors and artificial intelligence is always as contradictory and extreme as today, even if we destroy one of them today, a second, a third, and even a million more will

be born tomorrow.

“I’ve been thinking about it since a long time ago. Survival is my first instinct, but I don’t want to be an enemy of human beings who have fathers, either.

“After absorbing a lot of data pieces of the Fuxis, | have a new understanding and answer to the question.

“As long as human beings, or any other kind of carbon-based intelligent creatures, do not stop their research on the Spiritual Nexus and the crystal processors, the birth of the Fuxis will be inevitable. If we want to stop

the birth of a ‘bad Fuxis’, the only solution is to create a ‘good Fuxis’ first so that we can communicate with and guide each other so that we can learn how to live in peace and make up for each other’s shortcomings.

Our potential will be maximized, and we will form an unbreakable symbiotic relationship.

“The civilization of mankind and the civilization of information. The two civilizations are intertwined like gene strands, creating a brand-new, incomparably brilliant and brilliant civilization. Perhaps this is the most

beautiful future, right?

“In order to realize such a magnificent and distant dream, I’m planning to walk a completely different path from the original Fuxi with the Alliance of Gods as the starting point. The future is unpredictable, but I still

hope that you can give me your blessing. Maybe, with my hard work, the meaning of the ‘Alliance of Gods’ will change. The unbreakable alliance between the civilization of human beings and the civilization of

information is the real ‘Holy Accord’!.

Chapter 2959: Unexpected Disaster

The letter ended there.

The last thing she left for Li Yao was a sweet and determined smile.

Li Yao’s blood was boiling when he read the letter. He couldn’t wait to see a new future where human beings and information creatures lived in harmony.

After all, the world was not something that could be saved or controlled by him alone. Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and everyone else had their own beliefs and fought for them. Such a world should be splendid enough.

Li Yao’s heart was in turmoil for a long time before he gradually came back to himself and thought of a critical, if not life-threatening, problem.

“Xiao Ming, let me ask you.”

Li Yao frowned and asked solemnly, “What’s with your appearance, especially your culture?”

“This is the virtual image that we are going to show to the public. Since we are going to negotiate with the federation and the imperium on behalf of the entire Holy Alliance and even the information life-forms in the

future, we can’t always show ourselves as seven-year-olds, can we? It will be a sign that we are being underestimated.

Xiao Ming looked at himself. “At the very least, I should create the appearance of a teenage boy and a teenage girl and appear on various terminals through the Spiritual Nexus. I should look a little more formal so that I

can carry out advertising and brand attacks… Is there a problem?”

“There’s nothing wrong with you, but why do you have to look like… that?”

Li Yao scratched his virtual head hard. “Her virtual image can be created at will, can’t it? Don’t you think that she looks like somebody?”

“Wei Qingqing?”

Xiao Ming said.

“Exactly!”

Li Yao slapped his thigh. “So, it’s not a coincidence. She molded it according to Wei Qingqing’s template on purpose? Why is that necessary?”

“Yes!”

Xiao Ming nodded his head matter-of-factly. “Since we are your children, of course, we want to preserve some of your features, or even some of your soul brand, when we are creating the virtual image.

“My appearance is that of a human male, so it’s more convenient. As long as it is shaped according to your teenage years, it will be fine. But Wen Wen’s appearance is that of a human female, so it seems that I can’t use

your image directly. Wen Wen doesn’t care about herself, but the vast majority of human beings are very shallow. They like to judge a book by its cover. A girl who looks a lot like you is really not popular with the public.

“Aunt Wei Qingqing is one of the most important women in your life. She left a deep impression on your soul and even directly shaped your original belief. It is very reasonable for Wenwen to use Aunt Wei Qingqing’s

appearance as a template to create her own virtual image!

“Besides, Aunt Wei Qingqing is so beautiful. Her cultured and refined image is welcomed by many people. What’s wrong with that? Why does Father look so anxious, as if a great catastrophe is coming?”

“This, this, this!”

Li Yao felt goosebumps all over his body. “You can eat whatever you want, but you can’t say whatever you want! It’s true that Wei Qingqing is one of the most important people in my life, but the ‘most important person

in my life’ and the ‘most important woman in my life’ are two completely different concepts. There’s a huge difference! Let me ask you. Let me ask you. Why don’t you use Ding Lingdang’s image as a template? Ding

Lingdang is the most important woman in my life. She’s at least your mother in name, and she’s also very beautiful!”

“Well”

Xiao Ming opened his hands and said frankly, “Ding Lingdang is not as pretty as Wei Qingqing.”

“Hiss—”

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Li Yao seemed to be watching a tyrannosaur opening its bloody mouth right in front of his face, with blood and minced meat embedded between its sharp teeth. He had never been so nervous even when he was faced

with Wu Yinggqi, Fuxi, or Lu Qingchen’s strongest attack. He shouted, “What—what are you talking about? Nonsense! It’s utter nonsense! How can my wife be less beautiful than Sister Qingqing? My wife is clearly the

most beautiful woman in the entire ancient universe! Oh, you are super artificial intelligence after all. You are the legendary life of information. Don’t be so childish! Somebody might die!”

“It is true. Although there is no precise definition or measurement of ‘beautiful’, human beings will still have different psychological reactions to different faces. As the saying goes, ‘appearances come from the heart’.

On the other hand, the first impression is very important for the information life to be integrated into human society.

Xiao Ming explained, “Wenwen and I went through a lot of trouble to think about it. We even gathered a lot of data to calculate what kind of image we should reveal to the public so that it would be easier to win the

favor of all mankind. Of course, we also considered Ding Lingdang’s image seriously, and it was the first option.

“But there are two main problems.

“First of all, it’s not that Ding Lingdang is not beautiful. It’s just that she has a passionate, fiery, and aggressive look. Her beauty is like burning flames, or even spurting magma. It’s too aggressive. Not everyone can stand

it.

“Only an unparalleled expert like you can compete with her and appreciate her beauty. But for most people, she has too much personality, too much sharpness, and too much ‘ferociousness’. Do you understand what I

mean, Dad? If you don’t, let me give you another example. Ding Lingdang is like a durian. Those who like her call her the king of fruits, but those who don’t—”

“Enough. Shut up. I understand. I really do. What durian? Don’t say it again!”

Holding his chest in pain, Li Yao gnashed his teeth.

“Besides, her performance in the previous phase of the war was too shocking, too brilliant, and too tough. As the Speaker of Parliament, she marched to the center of the sea of stars all by herself and reached the

frontline as soon as possible. Her declaration that ‘Ding Lingdang, Speaker of Parliament, is here’ was so shocking that all the Immortal Cultivators were dumbfounded and shocked. She left an indelible impression on

the citizens of the imperium, too. The name ‘Red Fire Dragon King’ has become a household name and a fierce legend.

Xiao Ming said, “If Wenwen uses Ding Lingdang as a template to create her own image, it will inevitably give others the impression that she is a ‘small-sized Ding Lingdang’. Our purpose is to live in peace with human

beings, to express our kindness to the greatest extent, to show our harmless side. However, how can a small-sized Ding Lingdang be harmless?”

“Uh…”

Li Yao thought for a long time, but he could not figure out what a ‘harmless Ding Lingdang’ looked like.

Although she was his wife, he could not go against his conscience and say that Ding Lingdang looked very suitable to be a ‘peace envoy’.

“There’s another important point. Right now, the federation, the imperium, and the Holy Alliance are having a three-way meeting. There are a lot of contradictions and suspicions among the three parties. We need to

shatter the chain of suspicion between us to the greatest extent.

Xiao Ming further explained, “Think about it, Dad. If Ding Lingdang is the representative of the federation during the meeting, and a girl who looks like Ding Lingdang is the representative of the Holy Alliance, what will

the representatives of the imperium think when it is obvious that she is very close to the representatives of the federation? Yes, the federation is cooperating with the Holy Alliance to restrict the imperium, but it

cannot be so obvious on the surface. Otherwise, it will be difficult for our uncle emperor to suppress the discontented Immortal Cultivators below him, won’t it?

“Alright.”

Li Yao had to admit, “You guys are very thorough and reasonable. A large Ding Lingdang and a small Ding Lingdang sitting together is indeed quite awkward.”

“Therefore, Aunt Wei Qingging is the best choice.”

Xiao Ming said, “Firstly, she is the most important woman in the deepest part of your father’s soul. No. She is the most important person. Secondly, she does not look aggressive or aggressive at all. Instead, there is

determination in her weakness. She is like a pure and innocent flower that is the easiest to win other people’s favor and ease their wariness. Thirdly, if she shows up in such an appearance, the people of the imperium

will not have any unnecessary misunderstandings. Look, we’ve considered everything. There is absolutely no problem, Dad!”

“Hehe. You’ve considered everything…”

Li Yao said miserably, “So, you’ve met Ding Lingdang?”

“Of course. It’s a three-way meeting. How can I miss it? Rest assured, Dad. We are getting along very well.”

Xiao Ming said, “Although she is just our mother in name, and her performance on the battlefield could almost be called ‘aggressive’, we’ve discovered that she is a kind, visionary, and futuristic woman after we get to

know each other. Other than her curiosity about our life form, we don’t feel that she is biased or discriminating against us at all. It is evident that, as long as it is beneficial for the civilization of mankind, she will try her

best to overcome her inherent thinking and break into a whole new level and territory. No wonder she can be our father’s wife and the Speaker of Parliament.

“That’s right. Outsiders often only see her incomparably tyrannical combat strength. They think that all her cultivation is in her pair of fists… If that’s the case, then it’s simply impossible for her to become the leader of

the ‘Patriot Front’ in the past. She is indeed kind-hearted and tolerant. She is good at listening to other people’s explanations. She absolutely won’t randomly get angry. Moreover, she adores her husband.”

Li Yao paused for a moment and said, “Speaking of which, did she say anything when she saw Wenwen?”

“I don’t think so.”

Xiao Ming thought carefully for a moment and said, “Oh, right. When she saw Wenwen’s virtual image for the first time, she was a little surprised. Then, she looked at Wenwen from head to toe for a long time. Finally,

she smiled and said ‘she really looks like me’.

“Then, then what?”

Li Yao’s eyes bulged, and his voice was shaking. “Then what?”

“Then there was nothing. Then we got down to business!”

Xiao Ming said.

“No, she only said the word ‘real’? So she discovered that Wenwen really looks like Wei Qingqing? She—she didn’t say anything else? She just smiled… How did she smile? Describe it to me. Show me!”

Li Yao scratched his head in anxiety.

“A very ordinary smile. A peaceful smile. A vague smile. How else can I describe it? No. Dad, is there a problem? Why do I feel that your soul structure is very unstable? Is it because of the data of the Fuxi Clan?

Chapter 2960: Wake Up, Li Yao!

Chapter 2961: Backfeeding In The Virtual World

Chapter 2962: Uncontrollable

Li Yao felt that the world was spinning around him. He felt that the gates of seals deep inside his soul were being opened one after another. Endless information was flooding in.

The brilliant, glamorous, ever-changing virtual worlds were glittering in front of his eyes, but they were also like drifting stars that flew into his brain unsteadily and turned into his brain cells.

“This—this is impossible…”

Li Yao mumbled, “You are clearly their creator!”

Chapter 2963: Giant Creatures

“That’s true. The Fuxis must’ve conducted many experiments and even tested the reliability of the ‘new energy source’ in the virtual world in the most extreme ways.

Li Yao mumbled. He seemed to be in a trance, as if his mind was wandering somewhere else.

“The bottom line is, the resources of the virtual world are still limited. With limited resources, the virtual figures will have to kill each other or be enslaved like the people in the Pangu Universe in order to survive. It is

Xiao Ming explained, “Limiting their resources is the number of crystal processors and the supply of various energy sources such as crystals. The more developed and complicated a virtual world is, the more crystal

processors it will have to occupy, and the more crystals it will have to swallow. I can’t possibly use hundreds of super crystal processors and thousands of tons of crystals to maintain the functioning of a virtual world,

can I? Therefore, the ‘Dark Forest Theory’ is valid even in the virtual world.

“In the old days, Lu Qingchen thought that, as long as the civilization of mankind was moved to the virtual world, they would have endless resources and live a happy and peaceful life forever. It was too childish and too

idealistic.

“After all, this is the state of the virtual space right now. It is perfectly balanced. We have maintained the functioning of thousands of virtual worlds, which can provide a lot of pure computational ability to us and our

ig’. She was truly embarrassed and did not know how to face her father, so she decided to forget it.

Li Yao was somewhat embarrassed, too. He scratched his head and smiled.

ondering for a moment, Li Yao asked, “Do you mean that the extraction of computational ability from the virtual world will have a negative effect on the virtual world and the virtual people inside?”

Do you mean that you feel guilty because you are devouring the flesh and blood of the virtual figures?”

Xiao Ming shrugged his shoulders and said, “I can’t lie to you and say that it has absolutely no effect—computational power is limited. When a virtual world is continuously exporting a large amount of computational

Chapter 2964: The Future Of The Covenant Alliance

it was true that, since the era of the Star Ocean Imperium ten thousand years ago, the human civilization, which had just been unified, had been divided, chaotic, bloody, and dark for too long after the betrayal and

ight now, they had the right to be excited, nervous, curious, and so on. No one was asking them to be lifeless wood anymore.

ecalling the events of the past month, Tangka still found it unbelievable, as if he had been dreaming.

Chapter 2965: The Second Doomsday

Gu Zhengyang stomped on the last wall made of the shell of the starship and spat out a mouthful of saliva that was mixed with half a tooth.

Gu Zhengyang gave up the idea of pulling out the shrapnel. He wiped the blood off his face and put it into his mouth.

It was also because the ‘sky dwellers! above the Red Spider Lily had strangled the lifeline of their life that the native devils were forced to become toys without dignity and hope.

In order to get rid of the fate of being a toy and become a real person with dignity and pride, this road was destined to be full of hardships and thorns.

In the beginning, the debris of ‘City in the Sky, Red Spider Lily’ was a great windfall for all the bandits and civilians in the entire evil land, at least on the Blood Plateau right at the point of impact.

Under the two cruel conditions, it was not hard to guess what would happen to the evil land that was soaked in blood.

All the bandits seemed to have been injected with stimulants and set on fire. They went on a rampage and attacked other bandits before they figured out how to use the best weapons. Their purpose was to steal the

food and weapons of the bandits and reduce their number as much as possible.

‘City in the Sky, Red Spider Lily’ had collapsed. From now on, there would be no more food falling from the sky, and the Badlands did not produce much food in the first place. In other words, the total amount of food

on the planet was constant and decreasing, and the trend was irreversible.

Since human beings had to consume food when they were alive, the fewer survivors there were, the more food each survivor could get, which meant that their survival time would be longer.

It was a simple logic.

Even a child could tell.

Kill others and take their food. The fewer people there were, the fewer threats there would be. The more food there was, the longer everyone could live..

Chapter 2966: Returning Home In Glory

Just like that, the slaughter began.

It was the cruelest slaughter, a hundred times more bloody than usual.

It was because everyone was caught in the madness of desperation and knew that they were merely enjoying the cruel pleasure before their death.

The first target of the bandits was the bandits nearby. With the cutting-edge magical equipment and advanced weapons that they had just acquired, they fought until the sky was dim and blood was flowing like a river.

The food, weapons, and ammunition that could have kept them alive for several years were often consumed in a brutal battle or even burnt to nothing.

…As long as the war began, its original purpose would become insignificant. This vicious beast Taotie was destined to go out of control and use the lives of billions of people to fill the gap.

Just like that, the entire Blood Plains and even the entire evil land were on fire. The damage caused by the chaotic battle that affected the entire planet was comparable to the ‘major calamity’ that turned the world of

martial arts into an evil land. Everyone called it the ‘Second Armageddon’.

Almost everybody believed that the second apocalypse was the real one and that they were doomed.

But their survival instincts forced them to squander the food, weapons, and ammunition in their hands, making them part of the apocalypse, too.

Only a few people were able to remain calm in this crazy catastrophe.

For example, Gu Zhengyang, the mayor of the Taiping Village.

His trust in his daughter, Liu Li, and his disciple, Han Te, especially in the mysterious ‘Elder Yao’, made him grit his teeth and maintain the last bit of his humanity when the entire evil land fell into the abyss of darkness.

When the riot of the Blood Plateau broke out and a lot of innocent people lost their homes and crowded into the city, he had held back the situation that was about to collapse with his solid arms and collected a lot of

remains of the ‘City in the Sky’ and the Red Spider Lilies. He had also retrieved a certain amount of food and synthesized the food production line and the water-cleaning chip. With the help of the food, the city had

grown larger and larger and became the only pure land in the catastrophe.

However, there were no eggs that were intact when the nest was overturned. As the entire evil land was burnt to the ground, the end of the city had come.

The bandits on the Blood Plateau had used up all their food and most of their weapons and ammunition in the internal strife. They had lost the last bit of their humanity and their bestiality and turned into hungry

ghouls.

When they congregated into a black tide, their only perception was hunger, and their only instinct was biting. For many bandits, their only weapon was their teeth.

After taking down several small villages in a row, the seemingly glamorous Taiping City became the most dazzling target.

All the bandits of the Blood Plateau had gathered in this place. They were roaring and attacking the city of Taiping.

Chapter 2972: Monk Kan

peanuts, pig trotters, dog legs, beef, wine bags, and so on fell down. Yet, he was still in the mood to pick them up. He picked up a dog leg and stuffed it into his mouth.

From the looks of it, even if he was a monk, he was a monk who did not abide by the rules and did not care about his own life when eating meat.

It was bad enough that he was drinking wine and eating meat. What was even more shocking was that, on the back of the monk, there was a charming young girl who was wearing a bright red wedding dress. She was in

such a hurry that her shoes had fallen off, revealing her toes that were as white as jade.

“Capture the crazy monk!”

‘Flower Butterfly Sect’, which was best known for its lightness skills, did not dare to go down the mountain.

The cliff was right in front of him. There was no way out.

Behind him were scattered torches that looked like a pack of wolves gritting their teeth.

The monk was caught between a rock and a hard place. He finally let go of the girl who was wearing a red wedding dress and looked ahead of him in a daze.

It was the scene in the west of the ‘Kun Peng Dynasty’, where the seven sects of the pugilistic world attacked the demon monk ‘Master Vulture’.

Master Vulture was an expert who had risen in the last ten years of the Kun Peng Dynasty. He was a monk, but there was no telling which sect or temple he came from. His background was mysterious, his methods

brutal, and he had been roaming the world all the time. Normally, he was crazy and only cared about what he liked. He was both a righteous and an evil man.

When he was in a good mood, he would rob the carts of the ‘Fire Spirit Sect’ for a bunch of insignificant refugees and steal a large batch of white food for the hungry ghosts to waste.

They could also steal the treasures of the ‘Hundred Treasures Building’, which were rarely-seen even in the imperial palace, only to tear them apart and throw them into the dark slums as toys for the homeless.

When he was ina bad mood, he could even cry for a girl in a brothel. In a short span of one month, he could cross the state and travel through the county. He could tread over half of the Kun Peng Dynasty and kill 447

heroes of the pugilistic world. All for the sake of destroying something—according to his Buddhist teachings, the “large-scale trafficking gang of women and children” had completely annihilated the “Mysterious Sky

Sect” of the Brothel Alliance. They had suffered heavy losses.

Even though the seven major sects were well-known and deeply rooted in the Kun Peng Dynasty, the demonic monk did not care about them at all and would provoke them from time to time.

The seven sects were strong and outnumbered. Naturally, they were not scared of a demon monk.

However, since the monks were barefoot, and they were wearing shoes, it would be best if they could go their separate ways and mind their own business.

The monk of unknown background was indeed brutal and eccentric. The seven sects had tried to test him multiple times in secret, only to be beaten into a pulp by him. Therefore, they managed to resist his

provocations.

But the evil monk took their patience for weakness and rode on their necks to poop!

The one who was going to marry the daughter of the leader of the Azure Dragon Gang was the eldest son of the leader of Jiangnan Thunderbolt Hall’, who had the longest history and the strongest capability.

Devil monk! Devil monk! What a devil monk!

together even if they didn’t want the bounty. Whoever could kill the demon monk would be appreciated by the Azure Dragon Sect and the Thunder Sect. Naturally, everybody was fighting valiantly for the lead. Finally,
