“Thunderbolt Crossbows” that were not even equipped in the army. Even if the demon monk had superb skills, he would still be doomed.

On the mountainside, the heroes were either gritting their teeth, glaring, or laughing weirdly.

You are dragging me into your trouble!”

change it. My conscience is clear. Good luck to you!”

the rules secretly once in a while, is it?”

Master Vulture chuckled. He picked up a golden leaf, crushed it in his palm, and tossed it into the cliff.

An unbelievable thing happened!

The shattered dry leaves turned into a zigzagging, warm, golden pathway between the mist and the wind!

Master Vulture smiled. “This is as far as I can help you.”

The bride had not accepted the fact that the monk was a god and asked subconsciously.

Master Vulture looked at the sky, his dark eyes reflecting the dark night sky as if he had seen something higher through the darkness. He mumbled, “Over the past few years, I’ve remembered more and more things

and… who I am. It seems that my destiny with the world has come to an end. It’s time for me to go.”

The heroes of Jianghu all cursed. Burning arrows rained down around the monk.

The wind was blowing, making the monk’s wide robe flutter. Flames were soaring to the sky, making him look like a weird Buddha.

Looking at the furious crowds, Master Vulture grinned and suddenly did something that dumbfounded everyone.

He actually tore off his monk robe and took out the guy. He faced the crowd below and urinated to his heart’s content!.

Chapter 2973: A Rainbow

“Well…”

The entire Blue Wolf Mountain was silent. The only sounds were the blowing wind and Master Vulture’s pee.

The heroes of the world and the heroes who had killed countless people were all shocked speechless by his boldness.

The wind was blowing from top to bottom, blowing in the direction of the heroes. The stink of urine assaulted their faces, mixed with a few stinky raindrops. The faces of the heroes turned from pale to green, from

green to purple, and from purple to black.

The monk was not in a hurry to take a long piss. He tied his belt slowly and looked at the infuriated heroes with a smile, as if he had no idea how miserable his death would be.

“That’s—that’s outrageous!”

“This is crazy! This is crazy! This demonic monk is really crazy!”

“This is the greatest shame! This is the greatest shame! This evil monk insulted the entire world with his own strength! How could he dare to do this? How could he dare!?”

The warriors were all shaking in fury. Fifty Thunderbolt Crossbows, hundreds of strong bows, and shining sabers and swords aimed at Master Spirit Vulture coldly.

“How dare you be so presumptuous when you are about to be killed? Die, demon monk!”

Everybody drew close and roared.

Master Vulture was still smiling casually.

Dark clouds suddenly rolled in the sky. Lightning flashed and thunder rumbled. A torrential rain was about to fall.

Master Vulture wiped his face and suddenly changed his expression, which was filled with unspeakable majesty and horror.

“Have you not realized who is about to die?”

The shout from the monk’s chest was even louder than the thunder between the heavens and the earth. It was as if everyone had been struck by lightning. Their eyes were filled with red and green, and their ears were

ringing. Without them knowing, they had fallen into an illusion. They seemed to be able to see the scene of the Azure Dragon Gang and Jiang Nan Thunderbolt Hall joining hands to rebel against the imperial court

several years later. The world would collapse, and blood would flow, causing countless catastrophes. After overthrowing the Kun Peng Dynasty, they would kill each other and turn the bright universe into an endless

demonic domain.

The heroes who besieged Master Vulture on the Blue Wolf Mountain today would become the main force of the rebellion against the imperial court the next day. They were all awe-inspiring generals. But after they

committed grave crimes, they could not escape their doom and became dead bodies under each other’s blades, dry bones that had nowhere to be buried.

Other than dragging down countless civilians and turning them into ghosts for their ambitions, they would get nothing.

It was such a vivid dream that everyone was soaked in cold sweat. Their hearts were aching, and they could not tell whether it was a dream or reality.

It was not until this moment that he came to know that Master Spirit Vulture was an out-of-the-world expert who walked on the great path of Cultivation. Even the most renowned experts of the pugilistic world were

no match for him. For a moment, he was scared out of his wits.

Some of them were more quick-minded than the rest. They realized that the expert was not trying to steal his new wife but trying to gather the heroes who had committed crimes and enlighten them in such a way.

However, to everyone’s surprise, the tips of their fingers and the tips of their fingers were all shivering. “BAM BAM BAM BAM!” Almost a hundred Thunderbolt Crossbows, which had been fully loaded, were released

from their bows and rushed toward Master Vulture.

“Master!”

The faces of the crowd suddenly changed color, especially those of the experts of the Azure Dragon Gang and Jiang Nan Thunderbolt Hall. Their faces were twisted in fear.

Seeing that almost a hundred crossbows that could pierce through the city wall had passed through his chest, Master Vulture suddenly turned into countless colorful feathers that were carried by the wind and turned

into dazzling streaks of brightness. The streaks of brightness congregated into rainbows and soared into the sky, disappearing into the helical clouds.

In such an unbelievable way, Master Vulture turned into a rainbow and vanished from the Kun Peng Dynasty.

All that was left was the warriors on the mountain who were all kneeling on the ground and gazing at the helical clouds on the cliff in a daze.

The beggar had been begging at this intersection for twenty years.

n the city forest that was made of rebar and cement, there were many other people who were in the same trade as him. The passers-by who were in a hurry never noticed such a beggar, as if he was just a moving

stone on the roadside.

Beggars did not care about people’s gazes. They lived freely and did whatever they wanted.

n the spring, he appreciated the blossoming flowers. In the summer, he listened to the cries of the cicadas. In the autumn, he picked up fallen leaves and made them into whistles. In the winter, he watched the falling

snow with his head lowered.

When someone offered money, he would be all smiles. When someone offered two buns to eat, he would express his gratitude. When someone complained that he was blocking the way and kicked him, he would crawl

away without saying a word. There was one time when a few of his peers wanted to take his territory and beat him up. He would hold his head and hold his crotch, silently enduring. When someone else finished

beating him, he would vomit blood and curl up in his original position.

Over time, the beggar had become one with the intersection, turning into something similar to light boxes and electric poles.

Such an ordinary beggar who could be found anywhere would not have attracted anyone’s attention, let alone the major news media.

Before the out-of-control truck crashed into the little girl who was crossing the road, nobody had expected that such a dirty, cowardly, stinky beggar would be so bold as to sacrifice his own life.

However, the story of ‘the beggar sacrificed his life to save the little girl’ was not sensational enough. At most, it would make it to the newspapers of the city level, but on the network, where people biting each other

was not news, it was even more impossible to stir up any trouble.

The media and thousands of netizens were greatly interested in another topic.

The beggar was gone.

The moment he pushed the little girl away and was hit by the truck, he was gone.

That’s right. Not a single word was wrong. It was literal. Gone. Gone. Gone.

There were no traces of collision on the truck, nor were there any traces of blood or hair on the ground. The files of the police did not contain the files of such a beggar, either. It was as if such a person had never

existed from the beginning to the end.

There was no telling what was wrong with the seven surveillance cameras near the scene. Snowflakes were popping up all at the same time, so much so that they failed to record the scene when the beggar was hit by a

truck.

How was that possible?

Many people remembered the existence of the beggar. The surveillance video a few days ago showed such a person. Even the shocked truck driver and the little girl who had escaped the disaster confirmed that such a

beggar was the one who stopped the tragedy from happening.

How could a living person disappear completely? Even if he was killed by the collision, there should be a corpse left behind, right?

The story of the truck driver and the little girl added to the weirdness of the story.

The driver and the little girl both said that they had seen with their own eyes that the beggar who had been sitting at the intersection for twenty years had turned into… a rainbow..

Chapter 2974: Prehistoric Cultivation!

Strong Arm was woken up by clanging noises.

It was the beginning of Chaos and the beginning of the primordial era. There was nothing but darkness between the sky and the earth. Volcanoes were erupting everywhere, and black smoke was spreading everywhere.

The earth was shaking and the mountains were shaking, but there was abundant nutrition, too. The forests were growing at will, the birds and animals were running freely, and the primitive cave dwellers such as

‘Strengthened Arm’ were born.

Strongarm was the leader of the twenty to thirty cave dwellers in the cave. As distant relatives of the early human beings, they were all muscular and had long hair all over their bodies. Their hands and feet were

covered in developed muscles, making them more like upright gorillas than human beings.

The foreheads of the cavemen leaned back, but their jaws protruded forward. Such a skull structure not only gave them developed bite force, it also limited the development of their brain capacity and wisdom.

Therefore, they had yet to learn how to polish tools that were too delicate, let alone farming and animal husbandry. They could only forge crude stone axes, wooden spears, and bone clubs by chasing after prey and

collecting fruits every day.

Pursuing prey required abundant energy. If one was drowsy, their plump prey might run away, or they might forget to distinguish the tracks of the ferocious beasts, resulting in dire consequences. As the leader of the

cave dwellers, the sleep of ‘Strengthened Arm’ was a matter of life and death for the entire tribe.

Therefore, when ‘Strengthened Arm’ was woken up before dawn, he was more vigilant than ever. The hair all over his body was standing up like that of a chimpanzee, while he was purring in his chest.

The other men in the cave, or rather, the male cave dwellers, were woken up by the weird noise, too.

‘No Hair’, ‘One More’, ‘Pig Claw’. The men looked at each other in bewilderment, before they looked at their leader with dirty eyes and gestured to ‘Strong Arm’ to ask what was going on.

Ding! Ding! Ding! Dong! The sounds were coming from the deepest part of the cave. At the end of the darkness, feeble flames were glittering!

The cave was warm, narrow, and rugged. It was the best shelter for the cave dwellers.

However, the cave slanted downwards and sank deeper and deeper. There was nothing at the end of the cave, which was not suitable for survival. It was even more unlikely that a ‘divine tree’ was hit by lightning and

set ablaze.

These cavemen were still exploring the mysteries of fire-making and had an instinctive curiosity and reverence for flames.

Although the fire was feeble, it was enough to stimulate the pupils of all the cave dwellers, whose pupils constricted to the size of needles.

Strong Arm rubbed his sharp canine teeth and picked up the slightly polished stone axe. Gesturing, he crawled to the end of the cave with the male cave dwellers.

Very soon, they saw an exquisitely-made torch that was made of pine cones, animal oil, and bone sticks and far more advanced than any torch in the modern era. It was burning for a long time without being

extinguished. There was barely any smoke, and it was emitting a very pleasant smell.

The flames stung their eyes and made their pores constrict. Their muscles were rigid, and they found it hard to breathe. Even ‘Strong Arm’, the strongest and boldest leader, almost exclaimed and ran away in fear.

But what surprised them even more was the thin, small figure illuminated by the flickering flames.

“It’s Crooked Neck!”

‘One More’ who had six fingers on his right hand waved his hands and gestured to ‘Strong Arm’ to ease their anxiety.

The early cavemen did not have the ability to speak yet. Their coarse throats could only utter weird roars in short syllables, and the raging flames blocked their throats, making them too scared to utter any sound.

There was no need for ‘one more’ to say anything. ‘Strong Arm’ had already recognized him.

Crooked Neck was the thinnest cave dweller in this small cave.

If other cavemen were chimpanzees, and Strong Arm was a gorilla, Crooked Neck was a hairless monkey.

Such a weak caveman did not have the right to survive in the cruel primeval world.

However, although he was thin and weak, his running speed was very fast. He was also good at polishing stone axes, making stone spears, and all kinds of weird things. Most of the time, he could even smell the scent of

ferocious beasts to avoid the annihilation of the hunting team. Therefore, Strong Arm managed to keep him alive.

Other than being extremely thin and weak, Crooked Neck also had a problem that was not a problem. Every night, he rarely slept. Instead, he liked to run out to the wilderness where he could not even see his own

fingers. Craning his neck, he looked up at the starry sky, gazing at the brilliant stars in the sky. He was deep in thought for a long time.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Outside the cave, under the night sky, was the world of ferocious beasts. Sabretooth Tigers, Long-Haired Lions, the ubiquitous wolves, and the giant ancient pigs. There was no telling how they managed to avoid the

scourge of so many ferocious beasts.

Thankfully, the tribe of cave dwellers in the early years had not developed a primitive religion yet. Otherwise, alien species such as Crooked Neck would definitely be regarded as ‘devils’ by their compatriots and thrown

into the cliffs to please the gods.

In fact, according to the naming rules of the cave dwellers, ‘slanted neck’ should be called ‘stargazing’.

However, the barren brains of the cave dwellers did not understand the concept of nature yet, nor did they know what was blinking in the night sky. In their eyes, they were just meaningless light spots.

The combination of their short syllables and their ten fingers was not enough to describe the mysterious concept of ‘star’. Therefore, they could only call the thin fellow ‘crooked neck’ out of habit.

All the cave dwellers, including ‘Strengthened Arm’, knew that ‘Crooked Neck’ was a different species in the tribe. But no one had expected that ‘Crooked Neck’ would be so weird. He was doing something that no one

had ever seen, could not describe, and could not understand under the illumination of the fire.

He was drawing.

At first, he carved rough lines on the limestone with the sharp stone spears. Then, he smeared the paint that was made of all kinds of ores and added clean water, blood, and oil. A primitive, crude, rough, and full of

vitality painting appeared in front of the cavemen.

In the dark and chaotic sea of thoughts of the cave dwellers, there was still no space for arts, religions, or imagination. As the leader, Strong Arm should’ve stopped ‘Crooked Neck’ immediately, but his throat and his

strong arms were grasped by an invisible force. He could not shout or wave his arms. He could only watch in a daze.

While he was reading, a stream of wisdom flooded into his brain through his fingers like a clear spring, making him vaguely understand the meaning of the painting.

At first, Crooked Neck drew a lot of little people below the murals. These little people were waving stone axes and spears as if they were chasing… boars and bison. That was their daily life, and that represented them!

Then, Crooked Neck drew a lot of dots on the top of the murals. The dots were surrounded by radial lines, which were the things hanging high in the sky: the sun, the moon, and the stars.

In the end, ‘crooked necks’ drew a lot of dotted lines from the little people who represented them, leading to the sun, moon, and stars in the sky. At the end of the dotted lines, many arrows were drawn.

What did it mean? Did it mean that the cave dwellers who had just evolved from mud could fly to the sun, the moon, and the stars someday?

Strong Arm did not know.

He only felt that a lot of new concepts, new words, new pictures, and bizarre fantasies were flooding into the depths of his brain.

The sun, the moon, the stars, the sky and the earth, the universe, flight, exploration… He was looking for a new way to express himself. He would explain all the concepts to his compatriots and find a way to fly to the

sun with them.

Strong Arm was drowned by the enormous, deep, remote, and ever-changing world. Clear tears flowed out of the corner of his dirty eyes. He stood where he was in a daze, and the stone axe fell to the ground without

him knowing.

His compatriots were ‘one more’, ‘hairless’, pig claws’… Everybody was the same.

Crooked Neck finished the painting. His ten fingers were already worn out. The last stroke was completely dyed in blood.

He turned around and looked at his compatriots, who were both dumbfounded and deep in thought, before he grinned. His smile was extremely brilliant, pure, and peaceful.

While his compatriots were still in a trance and unable to control themselves, he snuck into the shadows beyond the torch’s reach and walked to the entrance of the cave, avoiding his compatriots.

He tiptoed and walked very carefully, not wanting to wake up the sleeping woman and child.

Crooked Neck pushed away the rocks blocking the entrance of the cave and sealed it again from the outside. Only then did he step into the wilderness of dawn.

The sun had not risen yet, but waves of redness were already rising on the horizon, outlining the steep, magnificent, and shocking ridge.

The river was flowing. Everything was waking up. The roars of animals and the chirps of birds were echoing in the distance, forming an ancient song.

Crooked Neck reluctantly watched everything. Taking a deep breath, he licked his saliva with his curled hands and combed his hair one last time.

Illuminated by the morning glow, the hair all over his body was emitting bright red colors, and in the depths of the redness, there were vague traces of goldness. He was clearly a weak monkey, but he was emitting an

aura that was soul-stirring and could not be concealed by the world.

Suddenly, he stopped combing and picked up a flea from the fur carefully.

Out of habit, he wanted to pinch it to death. But after thinking for a moment, he curled his fingers and shot the flea into the boundless wilderness.

The rocks behind him that blocked the cave were pushed away heavily again. Strong Arm and the rest of them were shouting anxiously.

Crooked Head didn’t even turn his head. With a slight smile, he leapt up and turned into a rainbow facing the morning sun!.

Chapter 2975: Inner World, Outer Body!

The trilobite was about to die.

tt wouldn’t have died so early. As one of the few dominators of the Cambrian Ocean, there were few creatures larger and more ferocious than it in the primitive and chaotic ocean. Even if it encountered a strong

enemy, its solid shell was enough to provide enough protection. Besides, near the ancient corals where it lived, there were all kinds of food such as sea lily, sponges, gastrointestines, and plankton. It was quite

abundant.

if it wanted to, it could have continued swimming in the seawater comfortably, hunting plankton, and living a life exactly the same as its billions of companions. Its structure and the rules of operation of the world

determined that its species could live hundreds of millions of years in peace in such a form, at the very least, so that it would not be killed by a global extinction.

But since three days ago, the adult, healthy, and strong trilobite had given up eating and simply let its hard body float in the seawater without doing anything.

it was as if… it was pondering, meditating, and exploring the meaning of life.

Of course, it was absurd.

tt would take hundreds of millions of years and enough luck for all the creatures in this world to be born who could think for themselves. The simple nerves of the trilobites could not even be called ‘systems’. How could

they produce sparks of wisdom and thoughts?

However, through the complex eyes made of calcite, rays of wisdom were flowing out and illuminating the chaotic world, exchanging endless information with the primitive ocean.

For three days and three nights, the trilobite did not move at all.

Some of the brachiopods, cephalopods, and gastrointestines were cautiously wandering around it. They thought that it was already dead and could not wait to devour its rotting body tissue.

ight then, the seawater was fluctuating. The animals were running in all directions, only to be discovered by a tough shrimp.

The Anomalocaris were one of the largest carnivores in the Cambrian Ocean with the most ferocious habits. The largest Anomalocaris could be more than one meter long, which was even larger than the trilobites.

aturally, the krill was not scared of the trilobites, but the two kinds of dominators of the ocean usually had enough food, and they rarely confronted each other.

However, a trilobite that was barely breathing and unable to fight back was a different matter.

The krill shook its eyes and charged at the dying trilobite, waving its giant forelimbs and sharp thorns.

This was supposed to be a sure-fire attack, but the shrimp that he was so confident in had missed.

tt turned around and captured a splendid scene with its eyes. In the dirty seawater behind it, the body of the trilobite gradually turned translucent, until it was completely transparent. Then, thousands of brilliant

colors beamed out of the transparency and dispersed into one, two, and countless rainbows, dispersing in the entire ocean and the entire world.

Different from the trilobites, the archaea only had one day to live.

They lived in an extremely extreme environment in the depths of an ancient primitive ocean, near a high-temperature, high-pressure volcano.

n just one day, they would have to absorb the nutrition and heat spurted out by the undersea volcano crazily, produce polymerized peptides and proteins, and divide themselves crazily so that they could pass on their

life information and discover the most precious possibilities amid the endless changes.

Every second was precious. Every division represented a new possibility. It was even possible that a new species might evolve into a real ‘civilization’ billions of years later.

The methane mushroom lived in such a harsh, mysterious, and sacred environment. It meditated and meditated every 0.1 seconds.

Finally, when it sensed the restlessness of life, the division and dissemination of information, and the origin of civilization, in the blink of an eye, the methane bacillus emitted mystic rays that were even more dazzling

than undersea volcanic eruptions before they turned into rainbow ripples and vanished.

ot just those worlds.

Chapter 2978: Small Problem

One, two, three, four, seventy, ninety, a hundred, a thousand rainbows surged out of the translucent virtual worlds and melded into a giant swirl.

to come. This is the outcome of most virtual worlds. Maybe destruction is the norm in the universe. Every star and planet will eventually die, not to mention the tiny life that lives on the planets and depends on the

radiation of the stars.

“Life, fragile life, is really like dust and bacteria.

“But…

“Even if we are going to be destroyed in the next second, we will still try our best to continue our genes so that our genes will be tangled together and mutate mysteriously. We will explore the endless possibilities and

fight for one billionth hope.

“Even if our endeavors fail, somebody, somewhere, will succeed someday. Am I right, honey?”

“…That’s right.”

Ding Lingdang closed her eyes. Her bloody mouth bit Li Yao’s rough skin softly. “We can be destroyed, but ‘hope’ will not. Never.”

BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM! BOOM!

More and more shooting stars appeared between heaven and earth. The fastest ones had already hit the ocean and the earth brutally, raising tides hundreds of meters high, dust hundreds of meters high, and blast

hundreds of kilometers away.

Countless birds and animals had been killed by the flames and the blast. But the remaining creatures, including Li Yao and Ding Lingdang, the two tyrannosaurs, seemed to have been stimulated by destruction and

embraced each other more and more passionately, echoing each other to their heart’s content. The cells all over their body, especially the life seeds, had etched the meteor shower into the deepest part of their gene

strands and were undergoing the crazy mutations on a microscopic level, hoping to create a new life form that could survive the coldness and the extinction of creatures.

Maybe, this was evolution.

With the meteoroids falling from the sky, the earth shaking, the volcano erupting, and the dark sky as the background, Li Yao and Ding Lingdang, the two tyrannosaurs, threw themselves into it without a care in the

world. They held their heads high, swallowed, blended, collided, and continued to pass on their knowledge until the end of the world.

When Ding Lingdang regained consciousness, she seemed to be able to smell the strong scent of sulfur and the dust raised by the meteoroids.

However, she was clearly no longer a tyrannosaurus rex, and she was no longer in the ancient era when meteorites assaulted her.

This was also a desolate continent without a civilization. It was surrounded by endless primitive forests. However, she was not a bird or a beast, but a tree.

It was not accurate to call it a tree. More precisely, it was a rarely-seen super-large plant between a tree and a flower, similar to the ancestors of the Kuafu Clan.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Her roots were hundreds of meters below the ground and could extend to several kilometers away. All the trees, flowers, and bushes that seemed to be independent were actually part of her body.

She could clearly feel that her ‘roots’ were absorbing water and nutrition from the depths of the earth, and she could also feel the subtle changes of sunlight, humidity, and wind. She was adjusting every leaf of hers

precisely, either curling, relaxing, or shivering, in order to maximize the radiation of the star.

All kinds of ancient information had been recorded in her cells in a mysterious way. She seemed to have grown in this world for thousands of years, or even hundreds of thousands of years!

“Honey! Honey!”

Then, she heard Li Yao’s excited cry.

She had thought that Li Yao would become another tree next to her.

But she had underestimated Li Yao.

Li Yao turned into a swarm of bees, or rather, what seemed to be a combination of bees and butterflies that had gone through excessive radiation.

“Although the flesh and blood of human beings are the most perfect forms of carbon-based intelligent life, it does not mean that other life forms are not worth learning.

Li Yao danced around Ding Lingdang like a swarm of deformed bees. “Let’s say that plants are the first to walk on the path of evolution in a certain world instead of animals. What kind of interesting things are going to

happen? Come on. Experience the path of harmony between a giant tree and everything in the universe!”

n the long time that followed, Ding Lingdang indeed experienced a brand-new… mode of genetic inheritance and mutation.

The tree that she had turned into was not an ordinary plant. It was more like a brand-new life form that boasted both the characteristics of plants and animals. Her own bees and Li Yao’s were enough to form a

complete, self-sustaining ecosystem.

Li Yao picked food from her body and brought her food from afar. She shielded Li Yao from the sunlight and the storm and provided tree hollows as nests for the bees. They exchanged materials, energy, genes, and

feelings in a way that nobody had ever heard of. A long, long time later, Ding Lingdang, the giant tree, had expanded to the entire continent and even extended countless roots as thick as tentacles into the ocean. Li

Yao’s bees gradually grew into a quasi-intelligent life form that was highly social.

Maybe, it meant that a ‘civilization’ was about to be born.

In the following days, they also experienced the melding and harmony of countless forms of life in countless illusionary spaces.

They had once been two pieces of ore. Under the high temperature and pressure deep below the ground, they had pressed each other and absorbed the tens of thousands of elements stored inside each other’s body,

gradually melding into one.

They had turned into the nautilus and trilobites in the primitive ocean and even the archaea in the undersea craters.

Of course, it was not without the forms of human beings. In the different stages of history, human beings experienced love and hate in different identities.

After seven days and seven nights of reincarnation, their souls had received the deepest resonance and the purest harmony. Under Li Yao’s billions of strikes, Ding Lingdang’s soul had been tempered and consolidated

to the highest point.

Finally—

Countless dreams were dancing like butterflies. After a hundred reincarnations, they dispersed like fireflies in the sky and returned to the shuttle at the edge of the Empyrean Terminus Sector in the real world.

The two of them were still sitting cross-legged with their heads facing the sky.

But something deep inside their eyes was completely different from seven days ago.

Lying in Li Yao’s arms, Ding Lingdang was no longer as angry as when she was the ‘Dragon of Fire’. She was more like a satisfied red cat that had just eaten and slept.

“The mysteries of life are indeed endless.

Ding Lingdang was somewhat lazy. She mumbled, “I suddenly feel that it’s too little to only have this life. I wish that the so-called ‘reincarnation’ did exist. I wish that I could experience ten thousand worlds and ten

thousand different kinds of brilliance. Am I being too greedy?”

“Not greedy. That’s what I thought, too. Maybe reincarnation does exist.”

Li Yao paused and hugged Ding Lingdang tighter. “And one day, we will find a way to ‘beyond reincarnation’!”

“Alright. I’ll believe whatever you say!”

Ding Lingdang raised her head and looked at Li Yao with her watery eyes. “Hubby…”

Li Yao had a bad feeling. “What—what happened?”

“Nothing. I just felt that the past seven days and seven nights were too wonderful and too hasty. I felt that I was swallowing the fruits of my labor. I didn’t have the opportunity to appreciate the wonders of life.

Ding Lingdang said shyly, “How about we continue for another seven days and seven nights?”

“Huh?”

Li Yao’s face changed color. He pretended to be calm and said, “Good… Of course, it’s very good. In fact, I, too, feel that I’m not done yet. To tell you the truth, I’ve only exerted thirty percent of my strength. There are

still—there are still a lot of killing moves that I haven’t performed yet. It’s—it’s really not enjoyable. But—but…”

He was trying to find the right words when their crystal processors started vibrating at the same time.

Li Yao raised his wrist to take a look. A hint of relief flashed in his eyes, but he slapped his thigh and frowned. “Look, look at these guys. They are annoying us with boring things such as saving the world and benefiting

the universe. Really. Can’t they hold on for half a month without us?

“Honey, it can’t be helped. I really wanted to fight another 300 rounds with you, but as you can see, everyone is waiting for us to go out and take charge of the situation. What can we do? We can only sacrifice ourselves

for everyone. How about we make another appointment?”.

Chapter 2982: The Final Battle Has Not Begun Yet

There was grief on Ding Lingdang’s face.

However, when she saw the information transmitted through the ‘Speaker’s Special Line’ on her crystal processor, her face suddenly changed. She regained the seriousness and capability of the Speaker of Parliament.

Their eyes collided in midair, producing dazzling sparks. Obviously, the message sent to Li Yao through Xiao Ming’s intelligence network and the message sent to Ding Lingdang through the federal intelligence network

pointed to the same thing.

There was news about Lu Qingchen.

Today, the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance were barely stable. Lu Qingchen was the last unstable factor in the Pangu Universe.

Other than the life forms that he could hack into the Spiritual Nexus, the crystal processors, and even the human brain at his will, the five supreme fortresses that he took away were the ultimate weapons that the

Covenant Alliance had built up with thousands of years of resources.

The combat ability of the five supreme fortresses was at least equal to that of a main fleet. They boasted amazing damage. Although they were not enough to achieve any strategic goals, it was not a problem for them to

cause mutual destruction.

Besides, the continuous combat of the five supreme fortresses required a lot of logistic support. In other words, if Lu Qingchen did not want to see them turn into a pile of scrap iron, he would certainly cause a great

mess before the fuel, food, and ammunition ran out.

tt was not a trivial matter. It seemed that Xiao Ming and the intelligence network of the federation had assessed the seriousness of the situation and reported it to them as soon as they received the news.

Li Yao and Ding Lingdang looked at each other. They were no longer in the mood to fight for the Great Dao. Therefore, they returned to the temporary base of the federal expedition army at the edge of the Empyreal

Terminus Sector and jumped to a dark, desolate space zone close to it without any rest.

The dark space zone, which was extremely short of resources, was actually the starting point for the ‘Fuxi Fleet’ to jump into the Empyreal Terminus Sector and attack the imperial capital.

ight now, the place had been defined as a brand-new, independent Sector known as the ‘Three-Way Sector”. It had become a public area jointly managed by the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance. It

was dedicated to the cooperation and communication of the three parties. All the high-end interplanetary meetings had been arranged in this place.

The reason why they chose such a barren place was simple, too. Since it was far away from the stars and most of the celestial bodies in the universe, the radiation of various kinds rarely affected it. There was not even a

drop of spiritual energy that could be absorbed in the boundless space. Therefore, it was very difficult for the forces to ambush the army there for a long time.

if a conflict broke out in this place, they could only rely on the crystals and ammunition that they carried. It was like lighting a torch in a sealed space. Very soon, the oxygen would run out, and the fire would be

extinguished.

Building the place into a public area managed by three parties was quite ‘safe’ for the purpose of ‘peace and development.

At this moment, a super-large star fortress, or rather, a super-large metropolis, was being built in the darkness. No, because it was not equipped with too many weapons, it was about to burst into glamorous brilliance.

By then, it would be the headquarters of the ‘Council for Armistice’, responsible for coordinating the relationship between the three parties, ensuring the transparency of training and armaments, and even promoting

the disarmament and the exchange of cutting-edge technologies.

n the distant future, the ‘Council for Armistice’ would be gradually reorganized into the ‘Tax and Trade Union’. While maintaining the independence of all parties, they would explore the new way of unification of the

human civilization.

Of course, that was for later.

At this moment, the metropolis was still under construction. It was quite shabby and did not even have a name.

But almost all the experts of the Pangu Universe had been attracted to this place to participate in the crucial secret meeting.

Looking at the name list that had just been sent over, Li Yao was deep in thought.

The emergency meeting regarding the ‘capture of Lu Qingchen’ was initiated by Li Linghai, the queen mother of the Imperium of True Human Beings and the president of the ‘Empire Reform Committee’.

She had invited almost all the well-known experts in the Pangu Universe, regardless of whether they were from the imperium, the federation, or the Covenant Alliance. The experts had gladly attended the meeting,

too.

tt was quite odd.

Firstly, according to Li Yao’s understanding of Li Linghai, his foster father’s old lover was definitely not a selfless ‘interstellarist’. Although she was determined to revolutionize the imperium, she was a selfish Immortal

Cultivator after all and would definitely prioritize the interests of herself and the imperium.

Lu Qingchen had become a drowning dog, and he knew a lot of secrets about the federation. There were also a lot of secrets about the Covenant Alliance on the five supreme fortresses.

f it were as simple as hunting down Lu Qingchen, Li Linghai would definitely have left the federation and the Covenant Alliance behind and led the army of the Star Ocean Imperium on his own. It would be best if he

could capture Lu Qingchen and the five supreme fortresses in secret, so that he could get a lot of secrets of the federation and the Covenant Alliance and increase his bargaining chips.

Lu Qingchen was a stray dog. No matter how strong he was, it was impossible for him to resist all the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium.

But right now, Li Linghai had taken the initiative to leak the news of Lu Qingchen’s discovery to the Star Glory Federation and the Covenant Alliance. It was definitely not because she had suddenly become kind and

generous. It could only mean one thing—she was in trouble. The Star Ocean Imperium alone was not enough to deal with Lu Qingchen.

As far as the federation and the Covenant Alliance were concerned, there were countless troubles that needed to be dealt with in the surging new era. It was unlikely that they would go all out just for Lu Qingchen.

However, many experts and leadership of the federation, including Jin Xinyue, Bai Kaixin, the Fire Ant King, and Peng Hai, had come to the Three Roads Sector.

it must be noted that, although the three parties had ended the war, the relationship between the two parties was still delicate. Many remaining members of the four major families, who had nowhere else to run, and

the extremists of the Immortal Cultivators were all waiting for an opportunity to break the fragile peace. It was quite risky to come to the Three Roads Sector at this moment.

Li Yao believed that, at the very least, Jin Xinyue was a person who valued her life. She would not risk her life without enough benefits.

tt meant that Li Linghai had offered irresistible conditions in order to attract all the experts of the Pangu Universe.

Or, worse still, the damage that Lu Qingchen was going to cause might endanger the entire Pangu Universe and ruin everyone’s hard work. Therefore, everybody had to gather and resolve the last trouble!

Beside Li Yao, Ding Lingdang had just finished reading the briefing sent by the Speaker of Parliament’s office, too, but she was not relieved at all.

“It seems that the real final battle is not over yet.”

Looking at the interstellar metropolis that was getting closer and closer and the darkness behind the metropolis that looked like a bloody mouth, she mumbled, “It hasn’t even started yet…”

The shuttle landed slowly in the No. 1 dock that had not been completed yet.

Since the interstellar metropolis had only just been built, not even a formal meeting room had been established yet. Therefore, a temporary office was arranged next to the No. 1 dock, which was indicative of Li

Linghai’s eagerness.

A large batch of experts had arrived in advance. They gathered together and whispered to each other. In the tense and solemn atmosphere, there seemed to be undercurrents.

As Li Yao and Ding Lingdang’s shuttle gradually landed, all the experts were greatly interested and gathered around.

Ding Lingdang was still the Speaker of Parliament. However casual she was, there were certain rules.

Right now, Li Yao did not have any official posts. Or rather, he had too many posts and titles. There were too many of them from the federation, the imperium, and even the Covenant Alliance. They were in conflict

with each other and did not care about anything else. He simply walked down the stairs and grinned at his family and friends.

“Li Yao!”

People from the federal department, including Peng Hai, the Fire Ant King, and the director of the Department of Mysteries, all came up and hugged him warmly.

“Master!”

Jin Xinyue and Wu Mayan bowed to him dutifully.

“You are finally back. You seem… stronger!”

Yan Liren, the ‘Sword Addict’, touched his short sword not far away and nodded at Li Yao.

“Black Wind King!”

The people of the Star Ocean Imperium, including ‘God of War’ Lei Chenghu, greeted him with complicated feelings. Although they all knew that he was from the federation and did not know what to say about his

personality or his suggestions, he was indeed the greatest contributor to the war and had benefited a lot from the collapse of the Fuxi Clan. There was nothing they could do about it. He was just that strong.

Immortal Cultivators respected capability more than anything else. No matter how shameless they were, they could do nothing about it.

Behind the important ministers of the imperium, Li Jialing looked at Li Yao gratefully and nodded at him.

There was no need to say anything.

Even if he was the emperor of the imperium, even if he was the golden lion, even if he had sworn to surpass Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi and become the greatest genius of his generation, he would always be Li Yao’s

good brother.

“Dad!”

Xiao Ming and Wen Wen both lunged at Li Yao. Countless spiritual puppets made of iron and steel greeted him under the control of the boxing champion.

There was no need to mention Xiao Ming, but Li Yao did not expect that even Wen Wen would come close to her body of flesh and blood. He was overjoyed and increased his evaluation of the seriousness of the

situation.

Looking at the heroes everywhere and the heroes of the entire Pangu Universe who were all gazing at him in awe and admiration, Li Yao’s vanity was greatly satisfied. He felt that he had never thought that he would

have such a day.

However, such vanity was soon buried deep inside his heart.

After all, he was a very low-key, reserved, and mature person. He knew that the situation was serious, so he hastily calmed himself down and started studying the subtle atmosphere between everyone..

Chapter 2983: Venomous Like A Scorpion

The three forces had their own specialties.

First was the imperium led by Li Jialing and Li Linghai.

Although they had suffered heavy casualties in the twenty-year-long counterattack of the imperium, the revolution of the civil war, and the defense of the imperial capital, to the point that they had to be rescued by the federation at the most critical moment, the Imperium of True Human Beings was still the most influential force in the civilization of mankind after all. It was a standard example of ‘a lean camel is still bigger than a horse’.

Other than anything else, at the very least, the number of Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, especially battle-type Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, far exceeded that of the federation and the Covenant Alliance.

In the civil war, the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators belonged to different groups and camps. They restricted each other or tried to protect themselves because they did not know what was going on. Therefore, Li Yao and Ding Lingdang were in the spotlight.

But right now, the new trend had been established. Everybody could see that the revolutionaries were unstoppable like the rising sun. Naturally, the Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators would not go against the trend and insist on being buried together with the four electors.

Every Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator was a national treasure. Even though they had been working for the four major families before, they were not easy to be killed by Li Jialing and Li Linghai. One of them had turned over a new leaf, and the other had decided to let bygones be bygones. Naturally, everybody was happy and happy. Therefore, right now, more than ten Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators were standing behind Li Jialing and Li Linghai, as well as several times more experts in the high level and the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage. They were a great deterrent for the federation and the Holy Alliance.

Then, it was time for the newly-risen federation.

The Star Glory Federation was arguably the biggest winner of the Star Ocean Imperium.

Not only had they captured a lot of starships from the fleet of the Fuxis, they also had a solid base in the center of the sea of stars and a seemingly reliable ally named the ‘New Covenant Alliance’. More importantly, in the last battle where the fate of the nation was at stake, the beliefs of countless Cultivators had been strengthened and purified by the cannon fire and smoke. As the prudent Gui Shishou had said, countless Cultivators had made a ‘breakthrough’ to a higher level.

Leaving the middle-level and low-level Cultivators aside, the federation was no longer what it used to be.

First of all, Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun, two spectral Cultivators in the Divinity Transformation Stage from the ancient sacred world, built their tempered bodies with the primeval technology of the Kunlun Mystic Realm, thereby restoring their combat ability to the Divinity Transformation Stage and making up for the lack of high-end combat ability.

Then, there was no need to mention Ding Lingdang’s breakthrough. She had stepped into the ranks of the strongest experts in the sea of stars. Supported by her firm belief, she was on par with any battle Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator of the imperium.

To Li Yao’s surprise, a fourth battle Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator appeared in the federation.

It was ‘Sword Freak’ Yan Liren.

The guy had broken through the barrier of his belief with sharp blades after multiple bloody battles!

But it was not strange when he thought about it. The monster and his fourth sword could not be measured by common sense.

More importantly, all the Cultivators in the federation carried a strong sense of fearlessness. In the eyes of the Immortal Cultivators who valued their own interests, they were out-and-out lunatics.

Therefore, the experts of the federation, led by Meng Chixin, Wu Suiyun, Ding Lingdang, and Yan Liren, were as intimidating as the Immortal Cultivators of the imperium even though they were outnumbered.

As for the Covenant Alliance, theoretically speaking, there should be a lot of experts. Not just the Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators, even the ‘soldier bees’ in the Divinity Transformation Stage had not been taken away by Lu Qingchen.

However, Xiao Ming and Wen Wen were not willing to leave the impression that they could ‘manipulate the top experts. Most of the experts of the Covenant Alliance were still receiving treatment to awaken their emotions and willpower. For the confidential meeting, only Xiao Ming and Wen Wen were taking part.

It was enough.

Because of the special life forms of Xiao Ming and Wen Wen, it was very difficult for them to be completely destroyed. But when they went on a rampage, they could cause tremendous damage to the internal affairs of the imperium. They were essentially experts in ‘unrestricted warfare’ on another level whose combat ability could not be measured by the number of Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators or Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators.

Besides, in terms of combat ability, there was an expert in the Holy Alliance who was definitely the best in the sea of stars. He was known as the ‘Punching King’.

Although the Fist King was from the Star Ocean Imperium, his mysterious origin and unique life form made it impossible for him to submit to Li Jialing or the path of immortality. He was definitely not the same as the Star Ocean Imperium. On the contrary, he was closer to Xiao Ming and Wen Wen. They were natural equals.

Therefore, he naturally stood on the side of the Covenant Alliance and helped the two kids protect the ‘information civilization’ that had just been born.

In terms of absolute capability, the imperium still occupied more than 60% to 70%, but the remaining 30% to 40% was enough to reach a temporary balance.

Although the real world was not a fairy tale where the experts of the three parties, who still had a lot of grudges and conflicts of interest, could not turn their hostility into friendship all of a sudden, Li Yao was already quite satisfied that they could gather together and fight with words and sharp eyes instead of fighting until their brains were gone.

Right. Other than the three well-acknowledged forces, there were also some experts who were on the three sides and maintained subtle neutrality. Let’s call them ‘the heroes’.

First of all, Long Yangjun.

Although she was born in the federation and had a high position in the imperium, her unique background made it impossible for her to submit to anybody. She was free to do whatever she wanted. Nobody could force her to listen to orders.

Next was Boss Bai.

When he fled from the federation on the starship, he had cut off all ties with the federation. From then on, he had only one goal, which was to march toward the multiverse and the high-dimensional worlds. He was free, too, and had even sworn to be the freest man in the sea of stars.

Even if Bai Kaixin appeared in front of him, there was no way that the ‘Pirate Potentate’ would change his mind.

From a certain perspective, wasn’t Li Yao himself the same?

The concepts such as the Star Glory Federation, the Imperium of True Human Beings, or the Holy Accord Alliance were no longer enough to constrain him today. He was destined to jump out of the Pangu Universe and explore the mysteries at the end of the multiverse to defeat… the opponents that he had to defeat!

While Li Yao was observing everybody, somebody was observing him, too. Long Yangjun combed his long hair and walked toward him with a smile.

Li Yao coughed. He was somewhat embarrassed when he recalled what he had said to Ding Lingdang

Whatever he feared would come true. Just as Long Yangjun reached in front of him, a familiar hot aura appeared behind him. Ding Lingdang had finally settled the red tape and appeared!

“Huh?”

Ding Lingdang poked Li Yao’s waist and said in a voice that could be heard by the other party, “Isn’t this your ordinary friend who is as ugly as a pig and as vicious as a scorpion? Quickly greet him!”

Sweat the size of beans appeared on Li Yao’s forehead again.

Long Yangjun was dazed for a moment. He squinted and rolled his eyes, before he burst into laughter.

“Ah Yao, you really said that? Really, you are so bad!”

Long Yangjun stepped forward and held Li Yao’s hand naturally. She said in a sweet voice, “You told me that, in case your wife misunderstood our relationship, you would definitely tell her that I was as ugly as a pig and as venomous as a scorpion. I thought that you were joking and simply agreed. I didn’t expect that you would really say that. I’m so

sad!”

“…Huh?” said Li Yao.

“Hehehehe. It was just a joke. Just a joke. Oh, sister-in-law, please don’t take it to heart. Ah Yao and I are really just ordinary friends who get along better with each other. There’s nothing between us at all. We’re so pure!”

Long Yangjun held Li Yao tighter and tighter. Her big, peach-blossom eyes blossomed with an innocent glow as she looked at Ding Lingdang and said, “It’s true, Sister-in-law. Ah Yao is truly a rarely-seen good man. You don’t know this, but sometimes, when we get drunk together, he would say, ‘I’m a man with a wife. I can’t let my wife down. I have to hold it in. I have to hold it in.’ Hehehehe. That’s so cute!”

“…Huh?” Li Yao asked.

“It’s true, sister-in-law,” Long Yangjun said. “Believe me. Ah Yao and I have never done anything wrong to you. It’s just that I felt sorry for him when you were away, and I took care of him for you for a while. Now that you are here, of course, I will return him to you. Don’t worry. I know myself well enough to know that I am just a passerby in his life and an unimportant confidant. I will not disturb your peaceful and happy life.”

“…What?” Li Yao asked.

“However, sister-in-law, it’s not that I want to lecture you, but please don’t be angry when you hear it. I know that you are a careless, even rude, woman who does not know how to take care of people. Most of the time, Ah Yao is angry but does not dare to say anything. If you don’t take good care of Ah Yao or even bully him, I will not agree to it. No matter how awesome you are, I will stand up for Ah Yao!”

“… That’s enough. Who are you? Are we close? We don’t know each other at all. Who is your ‘Ah Yao’?”

“Alright, alright. As long as you’re happy, I’m fine with anything!”

Long Yangjun finally let go of Li Yao. She looked at him deeply, her eyes full of glittering emotions, as if she was about to touch Li Yao’s hair but did not have the courage to. Taking a deep breath, she bit her lips and said, “That’s all for now. Ah Yao, sister-in-law, in short… you must be happy!” Sobbing, she turned around and ran away, not sure whether she was crying or laughing.

Chapter 2984: Giant Of The Universe

“This witch…”

Sensing that the silent volcano next to him was getting more and more irritated, Li Yao’s face changed color. He stammered, “My-my wife, you don’t believe such low-level sowing discord and making trouble, do you?”

“What do you think?”

Ding Lingdang was silent for a moment. Then she sniffed. “Do you think your wife is stupid enough to have no confidence in me?”

“That’s good!”

Li Yao took a long breath in relief and smiled apologetically. “I knew it. My wife is the wisest, wisest, and the most righteous one of all. This witch’s acting skills are so exaggerated, and her lines are so hollow. How could my wife be fooled?”

“Of course not. I know that the only woman you love is me.”

Ding Lingdang suddenly leaned close and almost bit Li Yao’s ear. She said softly, “So, there won’t be a problem for you to fight for the Great Dao ten times tonight, right?”

“Huh?”

Ding Lingdang said, “What are you sighing for? Do you think ten times is too few, or have you realized that you don’t love me as much as you used to and that the competition for the Great Dao has become boring?” “No. It’s not like that. Honey, listen to me. I think it’s better that we go into the illusionary space and swim and blend in the mental world. It will be more exciting that way. Really. I’m going to be the best for you tonight, okay?”

Ding Lingdang: “Okay!”

“That’s good, that’s good. Honey, you are so considerate and gentle!”

Ding Lingdang: “Of course, the spiritual melding in the illusionary space can’t be lacking, but I also want the competition of the great daos between real swords and real guns. I can’t be lacking even once… You love me so much, so it can’t be a problem, right?”

“…” Li Yao.

Having said that, Ding Lingdang suddenly pushed Li Yao and said, “Alright. Let’s get down to business. Let’s go to Dragon Lady

now!”

Chapter 2986: The Other Half Of The Fuxi

It was very likely that the so-called ’emperor’s tomb’ had not been established by the Supreme Emperor but modified by him based on a vast primeval relic. Such a speculation was not a secret among the three major forces of the Pangu Universe.

The primeval relic that the Supreme Emperor used as his mausoleum was most likely the largest of all, with the most secret treasures and the most advanced technology. It even involved the mysteries of the four-dimensional universe and was the final resting place for countless Pangu Clan members. Therefore, it was also known as the ‘Divine Mausoleum’.

Naturally, a lot of marvelous weapons from the primeval era must’ve been sealed inside the mausoleum. The fully-armed fleet of the Pangu Clan, such as ‘Kunlun’, the most advanced starship of the Star Glory Federation, might have dozens or even hundreds of them.

If that was the case, it was not strange that ‘Fuxi’, a super artificial intelligence of the primeval era, considered the tomb of the Supreme Emperor to be a must-have target.

“Although there is not much evidence, the assumption does explain the strategic purpose of the Fuxi Clan.

Li Linghai looked around and further analyzed. “Otherwise, it will be hard to explain why the Fuxis gathered all the elites of the Star Glory Federation and attacked the Empyrean Terminus Sector in full force. It seems that they are trying to finish everything in one battle.

Yes. The sea of stars was too vast for a war between two superpowers to be decided by a single battle.

It was quite wrong to say that ‘Empyrean Terminus’ was the heart of the imperium, because the imperium was too large and many worlds were self-sufficient. The imperium had more than ten hearts, and the Empyrean Terminus Sector was the biggest one.

In the worst case scenario, even if the surprise attack of the Fuxis won the war and the Empyrean Terminus Sector was occupied, so what? Would the entire imperium surrender or collapse?

It was impossible.

The cruel social structure of the Covenant Alliance had determined that the Immortal Cultivators and the vast majority of the ‘original human beings’ were mortal enemies. Not to mention the fall of the imperial capital, even if the imperium lost half of its territory, the remaining half of the Sectors would not give up resisting. The revolutionaries and the families of the four electors would not raise a white flag to a bunch of cold puppets and fight until there were no soldiers left.

However, since the elites of the Star Glory Federation had been dedicated to the assault on the imperial capital for a thousand years, they would suffer heavy losses even if they won the war. How could they cope with the protracted war of attrition?

It must be noted that the rear of the Holy Alliance had run out of fuel and was on the verge of collapse. Was the entire strategy of the Fuxis all about ‘death after a moment of gratification’?

Obviously, it did not make sense.

Then, adding the ‘Ancient Tomb of the Supreme Emperor’ into the equation would be much more reasonable for the strategical purposes of the Fuxis.

Maybe, for Fuxi, the most important target from the very beginning was not the imperial capital but the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor.

Fuxi only had part of the primeval crystal processors under his control. Its database was incomplete. The coordinates of the mausoleum had been lost for a long time. He could not find it even if he wanted to. Therefore, it came up with the battle plan to attack the capital city. On one hand, it was going to cover up the fact that it had broken into Li Linghai’s crystal processor and stolen his secrets with the carpet bombardment. On the other hand, it was also going to stall the main force of the imperial army so that the imperial army would not have time to deal with the anomaly of the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor. In other words, the outcome of the ‘battle of the imperial capital’ was not that important. Even if the war was quite intense, as long as the Fuxis found and opened the Mausoleum of Gods, inherited all the heritage of the Pangu Clan, including an intact primeval fleet, would anybody be a match for it?

Of course, Fuxi did not expect that the federation would send reinforcements all of a sudden, not to mention that two weirdos, Li Yao and Lu Qingchen, would show up. It was quite a pity that they were annihilated before their ambitions blossomed.

Although the Fuxi perished in the sea of stars, Lu Qingchen had inherited most of its memory pieces and databases. Therefore, he had received the coordinates and the way to open the tomb of the Supreme Emperor.

For Lu Qingchen, who was like a stray dog when there were only five supreme fortresses left, the only possible counterattack was self-explanatory.

“So, Lu Qingchen went to look for the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor?”

Ding Lingdang asked on behalf of everyone, “Is there any evidence?”

“Yes.”

“If you have a detailed understanding about the rise, expedition, civil war, and destruction of the Pangu Civilization in the primeval era, you should be very clear that, since the great expedition out of the Pangu Universe, there have been two super artificial intelligences named ‘Fuxi’.

You all know what happened to it.

“Although the Fuxi is dead, Lu Qingchen has been reborn and evolved in a certain sense. Of course, he has to carry on his ‘last will’ and swallow the other half of the Fuxi inside the ‘black tomb’.”

Chapter 2987: Triple Ruin

Such a conclusion baffled all the experts.

The so-called ‘half-Fuxi’ was not bombarded by logic bombs, nor did it awaken its self-awareness. It should still be a typical ‘weak artificial intelligence’. In other words, as long as one had the permissions to control it, it could be swallowed easily. As the first successor of the ‘half-Fuxi’, it was very likely that Lu Qingchen would be in charge of such affairs.

Besides, the so-called ‘black tomb’ was a relic of the primeval civilization billions of years ago.

Although civilizations were not necessarily more powerful when they were older, from the perspective of probability theory, the rise and glory of the Pangu civilization had only lasted millions of years. Therefore, it was not strange that a few ancient civilizations that were even stronger than the Pangu civilization had emerged in the past tens of billions of years.

The creator of the ‘black wall’ could create an overwhelming black curtain that blocked the brightness of billions of stars and converged the coordinates of countless worlds. Its civilization was apparently of an unbelievable level. Even the Pangu civilization failed to fully comprehend its mysteries.

Therefore, it was not an exaggeration to say that the ‘black tomb’ was a treasure of a higher level than the relics of the Pangu Civilization.

If such an ‘ancient treasure’ fell into Lu Qingchen’s hands, the consequences would be dire.

For a moment, everybody was silent.

Hundreds of the smartest brains of the Pangu Universe started working at the same time. Very soon, somebody thought of a fatal question.

“Wait. Didn’t Lu Qingchen steal the coordinates of the Mausoleum of the Supreme Emperor? But the other half of the ‘Fuxi’ should be in the so-called ‘Black Wall Control Center-Black Tomb’. Are the two locations actually the same place?”

Jin Xinyue asked.

Chapter 2989: Remnant Nebula

Li Linghai’s impassioned voice was deafening.

Even though the experts of the three major forces had long passed the age where they were impatient and their blood was boiling, and even though they knew that Li Linghai’s instigation had concealed his purpose, their hearts were still surging with emotions and their blood was fluctuating.

That’s right. Expanding the territory, breaking the mountains and destroying the temples, using the swords in their hands to fight for a wider living space for their clansmen-for cultivators and Immortal Cultivators as well as any intelligent life, this was the most sacred mission and the most glorious glory.

Besides, Li Linghai was very straightforward. There was no room for rebuttals at all. Right now, the imperium, the federation, and the Covenant Alliance were all in the special phase of exhaustion and recovery because of a series of coincidences. That was why they could maintain the fragile peace.

But even Li Yao could not guarantee that the peace would last a thousand years.

The hostility between Cultivators and Immortal Cultivators, the wariness of carbon-based intelligent creatures against information-based creatures… There were too many contradictions. They were like crackling sparks that could ignite an uncontrollable fire at any moment.

In order to resolve the contradiction and resolve the problem of ‘limited resources’, the only way to unite the civilization of mankind was to march into outer space, crack the mysteries of the black wall, and reinforce their friendship with blood and courage during the protracted expedition.

It was destined to be a perilous road with little

hope.

But it was much better than waiting to be killed when all the resources were dwindling.

“How about it?”

Lightning seemed to be dancing in the depths of Li Linghai’s eyes. He glanced over Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, and the rest of them and declared resolutely, “Every injustice has its perpetrator, and every debt has its debtor. There is no reason for us to forget the serious damage that the fleet of the Fuxis caused to the capital city. Although the Fuxis have been shattered, Lu Qingchen has absorbed most of their data pieces. He is its heir. The imperium will certainly capture the culprit and make him pay a high price!

“The Tomb of the Supreme Emperor contains a lot of mysteries and dangers. The Star Ocean Imperium alone might not be able to explore all of them. That’s why I invited the federation, the Holy Alliance, and all the magnates in the sea of stars to join the operation with 120% sincerity.

Li Linghai handed the choice over to the federation and the Covenant Alliance.

But in fact, nobody, including Li Linghai, had a choice.

If they were left alone, the people of the Star Ocean Imperium would have to go to the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor alone to hunt and explore. The outcome would be the same as letting Lu Qingchen fool around.

If the operation went well, the imperium would capture Lu Qingchen and figure out the secret of the black wall. They would be at the mercy of the imperium again.
