“Lu Qingchen is a citizen of the federation. Before he left the federation, he had seriously violated the law and owed a debt of blood. The federation has never stopped hunting him.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Ding Lingdang said solemnly, “The Star Glory Federation will not allow such a dangerous wanted criminal to fall into other people’s hands. The federation will gather the best teams to participate in the exploration. “Lu Qingchen stole a lot of secrets of the Covenant Alliance, including the five supreme fortresses, and fled to the depths of the sea of stars. Xiao Ming and Wen Wen also said at the same time, “Of course, we are going to participate in the hunting and exploration and take back what belongs to the Covenant Alliance!”

“Very good.”

The responses of the two forces were within Li Linghai’s expectation. She did not waste a single second and immediately entered the negotiation phase.

Firstly, the coordinates of the entrance of the ‘Emperor Tomb-Divine Tomb-Black Tomb’.

The so-called ‘Emperor Tomb’ was also known as the ‘Gold Throne, Heaven’s Beyond’. Theoretically speaking, it was a very special world fragment or ‘Small World’.

The reason it was special was that it was not closely connected to the three-dimensional universe. It appeared and disappeared like a ghost island in a raging ocean. One could only find it by precise calculation or by luck.

Li Linghai had once mentioned an even more powerful example. It was like kneading a piece of white paper into a ball, drawing a circle on the ball, and opening the ball. The circle had long been shattered into irregular black spots and ink spots—these black spots and ink spots were the trajectories of the ‘Gold Holy See, Heaven’s Beyond Sector’. How could one capture the pattern of its appearance?

Therefore, even Li Linghai himself had not been able to find the entrance to the ‘Gold Holy See, the Firmament Sector’ since he left it more than ten years ago. It was not until Li Linghai became the leader of the revolutionaries and took over the imperial capital where he calculated with the super crystal processors that the most mysterious relic in the legends showed up again.

However, this time, it was not in a good location.

It was between two nebulae.

The so-called nebulas were one of the most common celestial bodies in the universe other than the stars and planets.

Ordinary nebulas were made of hydrogen, dust, and radiation. Their densities were extremely low. They were often dozens of lightyears in diameter, but their total mass was not even close to that of ten stars. Many places inside the nebulas were almost ‘vacuum’.

Such a nebula was not dangerous at all. Even if a starship accidentally entered the nebula, at most, it would cause some interference to the exploration range and communication between the starship and the starship. It was necessary to pay attention to the loss of spiritual shield and power unit.

However, the form and causes of the nebulas were ever-changing. Some of them were extremely dangerous. The ‘Ruins Nebula’ was one of them.

Such a nebula was formed by the violent spurting of materials and radiation during a supernova outbreak. Supernova explosions were almost the most magnificent and the most intense astronomical phenomenon in the universe. Even if the explosion only lasted for a moment, the consequences would still show up billions of years later, which were the ‘Ruins Nebula’.

Such a nebula was like a rampant flood, a rapid swirl, and filled with the high-energy particles, super radiation, and even stone belts that were spurted out when a supernova exploded. What was worse was that, if the supernova of the explosion was large enough, a neutron star would be left in the center of the nebula.

The neutron stars with high gravity and density were one of the most terrifying traps in the universe other than the black holes. It would be quite troublesome if they accidentally jumped into the gravitational ring of the neutron stars.

However, the abundant energy and materials contained in the nebula also became the source of nourishment for all living creatures. Many galaxies near the nebula received selfless gifts and became breeding grounds for intelligent creatures. After the intelligent creatures grasped the ability to travel in the sea of stars, they liked to look for the nebula to establish new bases, factories, and laboratories.

The first bucket of gold on the road to the rise of the Star Glory Federation, the ‘Kunlun Mystic Realm’, was built in a nebula of relics in the shape of a crab and provided endless energy with a pulsating neutron star.

The ‘War Heroes Sector’, the origin of the Imperium of True Human Beings, and several other Sectors such as the ‘Sand Barbarian Sector’ were all near the dazzling Scorpion Nebula. The radiation and materials surging out of the Scorpion Nebula not only nourished the creatures of several Sectors but also became an important opportunity for Li Yao to break into the Nascent Soul Stage and enter the Divinity Transformation Stage.

Then, it was not unusual for a nebula of relics to exist in the trajectory of the ‘Gold Holy See, the Firmament Sector’.

What was most amazing was that it was not just one nebula but two!

The two super-massive stars that were so close to each other passed their prime years at the same time and entered their twilight years helplessly. After their bodies gradually decayed and the most violent reactions came to an end, their size continued expanding, and their brightness grew brighter and brighter. In the end, in the most brilliant explosion, a lot of light, heat, and matter were spurted out, leaving a brilliant stroke-no, two strokes-in the dark, boundless universe, turning into two colorful, raging clouds and two neutron stars.

It was a rarely-seen astronomical phenomenon and a cosmic marvel.

However, due to the vastness of the sea of stars, the number of stars was an astronomical figure in the truest sense. Even if the odds were one billionth, they would certainly happen in the endless time and space.

The distance between the two stars was around 7.5 lightyears. They belonged to two galaxies that did not interfere with each other.

However, when they turned into two nebula remnants after the supernova explosion, and the diameter of the nebula suddenly expanded to dozens of lightyears, they inevitably crashed, tangled, clashed, flowed, swallowed, perished, and exploded again.

It would be like throwing two crystal bombs that had been embedded with lethal shrapnel into the same room. When the two bombs exploded at the same time, the resulting damage would be unimaginable.

What was worse was that, when the astronomers of the Star Ocean Imperium observed the ‘Twin Nebula’, they discovered that the chaotic turbulence between the two nebula remnants was showing signs of merging, as if a new star was about to be born in the collision of the two nebula remnants!

Chapter 2990: Battle Of Monsters

A lot of materials, together with super-heavy energy, would be condensed when they collided with each other at a high speed. They would grow larger and larger, forming their own gravitational field and magnetic field. Eventually, all kinds of planets would be born. Such a phenomenon was very common in the early years of the big bang. Most of the stars, planets, and other high-density, large-mass celestial bodies in the universe were formed in such a way.

However, since the birth of the Pangu Universe, hundreds of billions of years had passed. The best time to create stars and planets had passed, and the universe had entered a relatively stable mature period.

In this phase of the universe, most of the celestial bodies that were born under certain conditions had already been born. Generally speaking, very few celestial bodies above the planetary level were born.

It was a miracle that two stars exploded at the same time and spurted out tremendous materials and energy. It was also a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity for new celestial bodies.

But the conditions were just the conditions. Judging from the environment and the astronomical parameters of the Gemini Nebula, it was still very unlikely that a new star or planet would be born.

It was because the materials and energy outbreaks resulting from the supernova explosions were spreading in all directions. The materials and energy that collided were only a small proportion of the mass of the two nebulae.

Before the matter and energy reached the critical point of the birth of a new celestial body, two neutron stars were already right in front of them, interfering and tearing them apart. The new celestial body was destined to die in the womb. After several futile struggles, the mass of matter that it had barely condensed completely collapsed. It was not until then that the Gemini Nebula was truly stabilized.

However, compared to the boundless sea of stars, the enormous stars, the neutron stars, the high-density nebulas, and the radiation swirls, the starships of human beings were much smaller than dust. Even if they were not fully formed yet, they could still cause tremendous interference and damage to the sailing of the sea of stars, or even destroy the fleet of human beings in the worst case scenario.

Therefore, the astronomical environment in the depths of the Gemini Nebula was more complicated than ever. It was not suitable for a fleet of such a large scale at all. Only a few elites who were more capable than human beings could break through the storms and the turbulence and retrieve the glittering and dangerous crown left billions of years ago.

It was also the biggest reason why Li Linghai did not dare lead the army of the Star Ocean Imperium to enjoy the food alone.

If the army of the imperium happened to be engaged in two cosmic storms in the depths of the Gemini Nebula and suffered total annihilation, the imperium and the path of immortality would be doomed.

On the other hand, if the capture and exploration squads were lucky enough to break through the Gemini Nebula and enter the ‘Gold Throne, Heaven’s Horizon’, they would be somewhat relieved.

Li Linghai told the experts present that the ‘Gold Throne’, the Firmament Sector, boasted a very solid space structure. It might have been a shelter built by the Pangu Clan or the makers of the black wall. They were not scared of the wind and waves of the outside world. As long as they entered the place, they would not be hurt at all no matter how furious the high-energy particle streams and rays were.

Last time, when he was exploring the ‘Gold Throne, Cloud Realm’, Li Linghai had left a lot of exploration tools, weapons, ammunition, food supplies, and even a set of alert and automatic attack barriers. Unless the dead were resurrected, there shouldn’t be too much danger on the surface. Of course, this was only referring to the outermost layer of the ‘Gold Throne, Heaven’s Horizon’.

The secret treasures in the outermost layer had been emptied by Li Linghai a long time ago. There was no value in exploring them. Their target this time was to follow the footsteps of the Pangu Clan and explore the items left by the creator of the black wall.

In order to enter the second floor, Li Linghai and Li Yao would each need half of the keys.

However, for such an important base or shelter, there had to be more than one simple way to enter it with a key. At the very least, the highest clearance of the entire base should be controlled by the ‘half-Fuxi’, and Lu Qingchen had the data pieces of the ‘half-Fuxi’. Would he be able to sneak in through the key?

It must be noted that Lu Qingchen did not have a real body. He could carry the waves and sneak in. As long as he found an invisible gap, he could sneak in like smoke and open the door from the inside so that his soldiers and puppets could march in.

Making the best use of every second, he finalized the preliminary details. Over the next seven days, the federation, the imperium, and the Holy Alliance were all activated like machines that had been fully functioning. The most capable experts, the most advanced starships, the Giant God Soldiers, and the crystal suits, as well as the most abundant marrow crystals and strengthening drugs, were shipped to the forward bases in the Seven Seas Space Zone.

It was an operation that did not require any discussion or unified ideology. It was a level higher than the Pangu Civilization. The legacy and mysteries left by the ‘black wall maker’ might have enlightened the entire Pangu Universe and upgraded the three thousand Sectors into ‘thirty thousand Sectors’ or even ‘three million Sectors’. The significance of the operation was beyond everyone’s imagination.

In the beginning, many Cultivators of the older generation were hesitant and suspicious, wondering whether or not they would expose themselves by risking contact with the Pangu Civilization, the makers of the black wall, and even the Flood Tide Legion. Therefore, they were considering whether or not they should take their time and develop in silence for hundreds of years. But right now, even such concerns were gone, because Lu Qingchen had gone!

Even if other people were unwilling to make trouble, Lu Qingchen would certainly make trouble.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Besides, even if Lu Qingchen didn’t try, the Immortal Cultivators would certainly try anyway. It would be a life and death struggle.

Just like that, after only seven days, the three major forces of the Pangu Universe had finished an unbelievable assembly with the highest efficiency. Almost all the experts above the middle level of the Nascent Soul Stage had gathered in the Seven Seas Space Zone. The battle-type Cultivators who were the pillars of the space zone were all present.

All the starships, giant soldiers, crystal suits, and other auxiliary exploration magical equipment had been repaired. The last cannonball and the last crystal had been loaded, too.

Right now, there was only one thing left.

At the periphery of the Seven Seas Space Zone, far away from the vacuum illuminated by the stars, on the orbit of the seventh planet, which was the largest in size, stones and dust. The stones roared and constituted a torrent of destruction at an extremely high speed, which flowed around the enormous brown planet endlessly. When the brightness of the star was completely eclipsed by the No. 7 planet, the temperature would drop below zero, and all the stones would turn into solid ice. When the intense radiation of the star appeared, the temperature would soar again, making the real ice crystals burn furiously.

The abrupt change of temperature, the destructive torrent of stones, and the enormous gravitational field of the seventh planet made the seemingly splendid, glittering ring a forbidden area for all life. Even the starships of the Star Ocean Imperium had to be extremely vigilant when they were accelerating with the help of the enormous gravitational field of the planet, lest they were swallowed by the ring and the planet.

However, today, an unbelievable scene unfolded in the burning halo.

It was a human being. Almost a hundred human beings appeared in the middle of the rings. They were rushing like bullets, colliding and exploding among the stones like cannonballs.

The human beings…

Each of them was tall and strong, with colorful, raging flames all over their bodies. Some of them were even wreathed in electric arcs that looked like dragons and thunderbolts. They were changing into various forms, carrying the dust and stones nearby into their own magnetic field and accelerating into fireballs and balls of lightning! Enormous planets, magnificent rings, and stellar radiation that looked like an ocean of flames… Against such a magnificent background, the human beings looked like the legendary deities.

With a closer look, one would notice that the equipment of the human beings was different and divided into three levels.

More than half of them were wearing delicate and glamorous crystal suits and carrying marvelous weapons that were embedded with marrow crystals.

One third of them were not wearing crystal suits. They were only wearing thin armor and mustard-seed suits that had been installed with a fully-enclosed life-support system.

Some of them were not even wearing armor. Their mustard suits were not even equipped with an airtight life-support system. In other words, they did not even carry any compressed oxygen with them and simply showed up in the vacuum where the stars were shooting, the temperature had suddenly changed, and the stones were flying everywhere!

Were they human beings or monsters, or the new direction of human beings’ evolution billions of years later?

No. Maybe in less than billions of years, under the threat of survival, the speed of evolution had always been faster than anyone could imagine.

The hundreds of ‘human-shaped monsters’ were naturally the best experts representing the federation, the imperium, and the Covenant Alliance.

It was the final training before the three teams set off.

The relationship between the three parties was more complicated than ever. Nobody could foresee the changes of the situation when they entered the mysterious ancient tomb. There were major problems in communication, dispatch, and commands.

Not to mention that, in the face of the primeval treasures that boasted immeasurable power, the belief of every expert in every organization could be transformed, sublimated, or corrupted instantly, turning them into a different person.

One second was all it would take for a Cultivator to turn into an Immortal Cultivator. An Immortal Cultivator could turn into a crazy animal. Even for information lifeforms such as Xiao Ming and Wen Wen, how could they know that their data would not overflow and that their underlying logic would not collapse?

Therefore, in a training session where everyone was trying their best to get to know each other better, it was necessary for them to learn more about each other’s capability so that they could better understand who was the boss and who was the boss. At the very least, it was necessary for them to understand the simple logic of ‘there are more than a hundred monsters here. Whoever wants to hide an ancient treasure and be attacked by all the other monsters will certainly die without a grave’.

Chapter 2991: Personal Breakthroughs!

The strongest of the three camps, hundreds of them, stood in the middle of the silent torrent of stones, which brushed past them like a meteor shower.

There were no rules, nor was there much protection. Everybody could choose their target at their will and unleash the most furious damage. Everybody could see the difference in their combat ability. The more skilled of them could certainly occupy the most advantageous position in the operation.

Although the war had not begun yet, there were still different levels according to past achievements, fame, and status, including the equipment on one’s body.

Among them, the experts who were not wearing any plate armor and only wearing mustard suits that were as thin as a cicada’s wing were the well-deserved super experts. Their killing intent was so sharp that it was almost tangible, and yet they could only lock onto their enemy. They did not think highly of the top experts on Nash’s side at all.

“In the previous battle, the fellow Cultivators of the Imperium of True Human Beings did not seem to be determined enough to fight. They did not encounter any exciting, soul-stirring duels. It is indeed regrettable.

Ding Lingdang took the lead and faced more than ten Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators of the Imperium of True Human Beings alone. Not only was she not scared, she was even rubbing her fists in eagerness. “Today, I can finally make up for my regret!”

They did not carry any communication magical equipment. But now that they had reached the Divinity Transformation Stage, they could communicate with each other simply with their mental power at their will.

Inside the planetary ring that was seriously jammed and in a harsh environment, her ‘voice’ was still so clear that every Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator of the Imperium of True Human Beings could sense it through the waves of her mental power. The Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators were all shocked, too, and did not dare underestimate the ‘Dragon King of Fire’, who had undergone a complete transformation when he just advanced into the Divinity Transformation Stage!

Ding Lingdang was a rarely-seen genius of martial arts in the past hundred years. Besides, she had made a ‘breakthrough’ in the cruel battles in the vast sea of stars. Her belief was filled with the desire for battle and victory, which far exceeded that of any Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator of the imperium. Her fists had been trained to an unbelievable, unreasonable level.

More importantly, over the past fourteen days, she had been engaged in a mental war with Li Yao for seven days and seven nights in the illusion space, tempering and consolidating her soul to the maximum. In the seven nights that followed, she had been engaged in a great, arduous struggle for the Great Dao that almost blew up every cell in her body. In the super collision of cells, flesh, blood, and soul, her body, which had been extremely strong in the first place, was pushed to a terrifying level hundreds of times beyond the limits of human beings.

A sparring partner who was in the Soul Splitting Stage day and night, through the competition of the Great Dao, would inject the essence of life into his body and cleanse his veins and nerves. Ding Lingdang was the only one in the entire Pangu Universe who could enjoy such treatment.

The exhaustion and lethargy of a division expert, on the other hand, was met with the rise of an unparalleled fiend! For example, if the Immortal Cultivators of the Imperium of True Human Beings were all burning, shining stars, Ding Lingdang would be the white dwarf, the neutron star, and the pulsar.

Or, the ‘total mass’ of the latter was smaller, but the density was thousands of times higher.

The outcome of the collision was self-explanatory.

Another thing that made Ding Lingdang let go of the burden and feel relaxed enough to devote herself to the battle was that she had made a public speech to the entire federation through remote video transmission yesterday, announcing that she would resign as the Speaker of Parliament due to “physical reasons”, effective immediately.

It was only natural.

The operation to explore the ‘Ancient Tomb of the Supreme Emperor—the Divine Tomb—the Black Tomb’ was extremely dangerous. There was a slim chance that they would be wiped out if they were not careful.

As one of the four Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators of the Star Glory Federation, Ding Lingdang had to take the risk herself.

But as the Speaker of Parliament, her death was bound to affect the functioning of the machinery of the federation.

The only solution was to end the Speaker’s career early.

Thankfully, her term had already entered its last year. She had announced to the outside world through various channels that she was merely the ‘special Speaker of Parliament in a time of war’. When the war came to an end, her mission would be over, and she would continue to shine for the federation in a more suitable position.

From the leadership to the public, everyone was well aware of her situation and had long been mentally prepared for this matter. New Speaker candidates also emerged one after another and began to make a name for themselves in the world of public opinion.

(If you have problems with this website, please continue reading your novel on our new website THANKS!)

Besides, the Star Glory Federation had been in the Warring States Period for six hundred years. The government had been prepared to suffer tremendous losses at any time. A hundred years ago, when Jiang Hailu, the Speaker of Parliament, was assassinated by Lu Zui, the director of the Secret Art Department, and the Supreme Council almost came to an end, the federation established a ‘shadow cabinet’ system. From the Speaker of Parliament to the ministers of the various departments, there were ‘back-up candidates’. Even if they were assassinated by an enemy, or if they were mentally deranged because of excessive training, or if they were forced to resign because of special circumstances like Ding Lingdang’s, there would be candidates who were qualified, respected, and capable enough to be replaced immediately. Therefore, right now, Ding Lingdang had really put aside all the restraints and concerns imposed upon her. She could wholeheartedly, freely, and unscrupulously release her combat ability and destructive power that was like a prehistoric ferocious beast.

She was like a flood that had been suppressed for five years and was about to drown the entire universe.

Not just the experts of the federation, the experts of the imperium and the Covenant Alliance had made plans for their future, too. They handed over their power to other people and devoted themselves to the unprecedented exploration.

For the Immortal Cultivators, it seemed inconceivable to hand over power to someone else.

But on second thought, if they could get the heritage of the Supreme Emperor, the warships of the Pangu Clan, and even the ultimate treasures left by the makers of the black wall from the ‘Ancient Tomb of the Supreme Emperor’, who would dare to refuse to return the power to them when they returned victorious?

On the other hand, if they were greedy for temporary power and did not dare to participate in the exploration, what right did they have to continue sitting on the throne when everybody else had returned with unbelievable adventures?

Even Li Jialing, the emperor of the imperium, insisted on ‘arriving in person’.

Otherwise, if other experts got the legacy of the Supreme Emperor, the Pangu Warship, and the ultimate treasures of the ancient times, how could he compete with them with his ‘Wu Yingqi’s legacy’?

Because of the uniqueness of the operation, the three parties of the Star Ocean Imperium, the Star Glory Federation, and the Covenant Alliance, as well as all the other heroes, had made a deal that they would abandon their past identities during the operation and assign tasks based on their capability. They would address each other as ‘Fellow Cultivators’.

Even if he did not consider himself to be the ’emperor’ of the imperium, Li Jialing, who had absorbed the knowledge of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi and sharpened himself in the defense of the imperial capital, was not scared of any opponent, including Ding Lingdang.

Not far away from Li Jialing was Li Linghai.

Li Linghai’s background was very similar to Ding Lingdang’s. They were both legendary martial arts geniuses.

Ding Lingdang had Li Yao’s help in her training, and Li Linghai had been possessed by the remaining soul of Black Star Emperor Wu Yingqi over the past decades. He had become Wu Yingqi’s ‘true disciple’. The legendary Dragon Emperor Cultivation Art, which had been strengthened by the Blood God, was extremely powerful!

Li Linghai’s capability could not have been more impressive, not to mention that he had been killed by her brother, Li Lingfeng, who was also a seasoned god, while she was talking and laughing

In the battle of the Gold Crystal Towers below the ground of Empyrean Terminus, Wu Yingqi’s remaining soul had burst out, overtaxing Li Linghai’s body and almost killing him.

At that time, all the specialists, including Li Yao, believed that Li Linghai’s body was going to be wasted. If he was not killed, his Cultivation would plummet, and he would fall from the Divinity Transformation Stage to the Nascent Soul Stage or even the Core Formation Stage.

Everyone had given up on Li Linghai, except for one. It was Li Jialing.

Although he was serious about Li Linghai on the surface, Li Jialing, who had lacked motherly love since childhood, valued his own mother more than anybody expected.

In the battle of the Gold Crystal Towers, in order to protect himself, Li Linghai had even gone against Wu Yingqi’s will.

If it were not for the deep motherly love that Li Linghai had for Li Jialing, Li Yao wouldn’t have been able to do anything even if he shouted himself hoarse.

Therefore, deep down his heart, Li Jialing loved Li Linghai and swore that he would let his mother walk out of the abyss and regain her former strength!

As the saying went, the mother and the son were connected by blood. Li Jialing and Li Linghai’s spiritual energy transmission and integration were not hindered at all. They seemed to share the same blood, veins, and nerves.

Besides, Li Jialing had absorbed Wu Yingqi’s soul pieces, and Li Linghai was Wu Yingqi’s true disciple. The two of them had been training in the same system named ‘Dragon Emperor Empyrean Terminus’. When Li Jialing poured his spiritual energy into Li Linghai’s body to treat his mother’s wounds without caring about the cost, the result was even more unbelievable. Not only was Li Linghai’s spiritual energy recovered day by day, even Li Jialing’s spiritual energy reserve was stronger than ever.

As the emperors and empresses of the Imperium of True Human Beings, who could enjoy more training resources than them? As long as they found a suitable training method, it would be too conservative to describe their progress as rapid.

His son had poured in the best Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures without caring about the cost. With the help of the cutting-edge technology, after countless days and nights of inhuman suffering, Li Linghai finally gritted his teeth and returned to his previous level, if not higher.

Those who returned to the peak from the abyss would always see many different things when they looked at the scenery above the peak.

Even Li Jialing and Li Linghai might not have been among the top three experts in the Pangu Universe, but the two of them, who both practiced the ‘Dragon Emperor Empyrean’ and were connected to each other, had unparalleled teamwork. They were definitely one of the strongest pairs in the Pangu Universe.

Chapter 2992: Sword Unsheathed!

On one side, the former Speaker of Parliament was confronting the Emperor and the Empress Dowager, and on the other side, some ‘non-human beings’ were confronting each other calmly on their crystal puppets.

Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and Wu Suiyun, two other Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators from the Star Glory Federation, had their eyes on them.

The reason was simple. Meng Chixin and Wu Suiyun were rarely-seen spectral Cultivators in the Divinity Transformation Stage. They did not have bodies of flesh and blood, either. Instead, they were controlling battle puppets that were armed to the teeth. At first glance, they were no different from the life forms of Xiao Ming, Wenwen, and the Fist King.

It was the truth.

What was the difference between ‘self-organized, upgradeable database’ and ‘soul’? Nobody could tell.

Maybe, the human brain was a crystal processor in the first place. It was a special crystal processor that used cells instead of chips as nodes for calculation. The spectral Cultivators and the information life-forms were the branches of the two different landscapes on the path of human evolution.

“I didn’t know that Fellow Cultivator Wu and I could wait until today.

Meng Chi, the spectral Cultivator, stood on the ring and observed the endless view of the stars in the distance. He was greatly touched. “We came from an era of darkness and ignorance. In our native civilization, the evolution of mankind seemed to be coming to an end. Nobody could escape the desperation of falling and rotting while they killed each other. In pursuit of a slim hope, we ventured into the relics, only to destroy our own bodies.

“At that time, we thought that all hope had been extinguished and that the hundred-thousand-year-old civilization of the Ancient Saint World, as well as us, was about to wither. We did not expect that there would be a silver lining at the end of the tunnel. The universe was vast, the world was splendid, and the civilization of mankind had so many brilliant changes. “Walking from ‘ancient’ to ‘modern’ and from the Ancient Sacred World to the center of the sea of stars, we met… ‘Little Ming’ and ‘Wenwen’. You are the representatives of the future, and you heard the news of the multiverse from the ‘Punching King’. You know the insignificance of the Pangu Universe and the vastness of the outer domain. Fellow Cultivator Wu and I have truly… broadened our horizons. We are overwhelmed with emotions!

“Then, Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, and the Fist King, please tell us about the endless brilliance from the future and the space zones!”

Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua! Shua!

Inside and outside of the ring, hundreds of spiritual puppets that looked like meteorites suddenly came to life.

This was the unique advantage of the Divinity Transformation Stage ghost cultivators and the information creatures. In terms of individual combat ability, they might not be a match for the super experts such as Ding Lingdang, but whether it was Xiao Ming, Wen Wen, the Fist King, Meng Chixin, or Wu Suiyun, they could command five or even twenty spiritual puppets at their will. One person was equivalent to a small army!

The ‘body’ of Xiao Ming and Wen Wen came from the essence of the Sanctuary’s research and development over a thousand years. Their combat ability far exceeded that of high-end crystal suits.

The ‘body’ of the boxing champion had been personally refined and adjusted by Li Yao. It was needless to say how formidable his combat ability was.

Meng Chixin’s and Wu Suiyun’s bodies, on the other hand, came from the Mysterious Realm of Kunlun and the underground of the Ancient Saint World. They were filled with a vintage vibe and a dominating aura.

Even the vacuum where sound waves could not reach was shaking because of their intimidating aura. Cracking noises were almost echoing nonstop. In a moment, hundreds of experts locked onto their targets. The atmosphere was so solemn and heated that the vacuum almost exploded.

“Then… let’s fight!”

Ding Lingdang bellowed. The mustard suit, which was made of special materials and boasted high hardness and tenacity that even a single crystal blade might not be able to cut through, was torn apart, revealing the crimson skin on its limbs. The ‘Red Fire Dragon King’ was the first to bare its fangs and crush a meteorite below its feet into powder before it lunged at Li Jialing and Li Linghai with the counterforce.

However,

She was fast, but somebody else was faster!

A petite and lithe figure, like a bolt of lightning, like a ghost and a ghost, arrived first. She actually leapt to the left of Li Yao after a few leaps and turns while stepping on the broken stones. She even shot a stream of sword aura at Li Yao very stealthily.

Ding Lingdang’s eyelashes, pupils, and heart were as natural as breathing. She punched out reflexively and blew the sword aura into pieces, as if it were an annoying fly.

But as it turned out, this was exactly their purpose.

The enemy had never thought that he could hurt Ding Lingdang with the sword aura. He was merely trying to lure Ding Lingdang into punching him and send him off with the waves.

The shattered sword aura did not vanish in the vacuum. Instead, it ‘woven’ into a soft ‘net’ under the amazing control of his opponent and blocked Ding Lingdang’s overwhelming punch. As if Ding Lingdang’s fist was the engine under his feet, his speed soared to the maximum. He crashed through more than ten stones and reached the heads of Li Jialing and Li Linghai.

All the stones that were smashed into pieces turned into powder, too. Surrounded by the surging electric arcs, they condensed into a long sword dozens of meters long that was both hard and soft.

BOOM! CRACK!

The vacuum could not transmit sound waves, but it could not block the fighting will and sword aura that were hundreds of times larger than the body of the man. Seeing the giant sword made of thunder and meteoroids, Li Jialing and Li Linghai both felt that they were struck by lightning. Even their firm belief in the Divinity Transformation Stage was shaking slightly. It was a strike that could even cut a starship in half!

The sword-bearer, on the other hand, did not pilot the Giant God Soldier. He did not even put on his crystal suit, nor did he unsheathe the magical equipment that he was most familiar with. He simply summoned his spiritual energy, crushed the meteoroids, and retrieved the materials on the spot. Such a sword of aura was already so powerful!

“Sword Addict’ Yan Liren!

Li Jialing’s pupils constricted violently. Gold brilliance was beaming out of his eyes.

Li Yao had once talked with him about the heroes in the sea of stars. He had praised ‘Sword Freak’ Yan Liren’s capability and thought that Yan Liren was one of the few real sword immortals who could ignore the gap between their Cultivation and kill the strongest experts.

“As long as Yan Liren has a sword, no crystal suit or giant soldier will be able to stop him. Even the deities might be slain by him!

This was Li Yao’s evaluation of Yan Liren.

Li Jialing was too young to believe that such a sword immortal existed.

It was a common disease in the human world. He had thought that even Li Yao was no exception. Did he really think that a Nascent Soul Stage swordsman in the closed and ignorant Ancient Saint World was a sword immortal?

It was not until this moment, when Yan Liren’s sword aura was shining in front of his eyes, that he finally understood what a ‘breakthrough’ meant.

The sword was Yan Liren’s life, his universe, and the devil in his heart.

“Little Emperor, everything inside the tomb, including the legacy of the Supreme Emperor, the warship of Pangu, and the ultimate treasures of the primeval era, are all yours. I don’t want anything!”

Li Jialing sensed Yan Liren’s determination from the unstoppable sword aura. “I only want the Supreme Emperor. I want the Pangu Clan. I want the makers of the black wall. I want the Flood Tide Legion. I want the real Flood Tide to be slashed down by my sword one by one!

Such boldness, such madness, such a sword immortal!

Li Jialing was both surprised and excited. While he admired Yan Liren, he also maximized his fighting will. Instead of hesitating and dodging, he charged at Yan Liren’s sword aura.

“I am… the Child of the Universe. No force can make me retreat and stop me!

BOOM!

Surging purple flames were surging around Li Jialing like a volcanic eruption. In the middle of the purple flames, a golden lion soared into the sky and lunged at Yan Lili’s sword aura. “Ultimate Purple Dragon, Torrential Emperor Flames!” The two terrifying forces collided brutally, resulting in 99 dazzling ripples. All the meteoroids within several square kilometers were shattered, and the dust was all burnt into a colorful fireball. A golden lion seemed to be fighting against countless electric arcs inside the fireball. The mustard suits of the two super experts were immediately riddled with holes.

The two of them retreated at the same time.

It was not because of the blast, but because they were preparing for an even more intense collision.

While they were retreating, Ding Lingdang and Li Linghai rushed close, too. The Empress Dowager and the former Speaker of Parliament dashed into the fire ball that had not been extinguished yet without any hesitation. The fireball was shattered in the blink of an eye and turned into the shadows of swords and sabers between them.

The Red Dragon King versus the Ultimate Purple Dragon, and the Red Dragon versus the True Dragon. In the senses of the experts nearby, two awe-inspiring, world-swallowing dragons seemed to have appeared and were about to tear apart the aura of the No. 7 planet. For a moment, before the two of them were able to determine the winner, the experts nearby were already sweating in fear.

“Wasn’t it said that Ding Lingdang had just broken through to the Divinity Transformation Stage on the battlefield not long ago? She should be a newcomer in the circle of Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators, and her experience and spiritual energy reserve are both lacking. How could she possess such appalling destructive power? Even those veteran Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators were blown up by her with a single punch?”

“Also, what’s wrong with Her Majesty? Wasn’t it said that she was over-exhausted in the battle of the Gold Crystal Tower and her Cultivation fell to the Nascent Soul Stage, with no hope of returning to the Divinity Transformation Stage in her life? How long has it been? Her capability has been fully restored, if not improved!

Countless experts were greatly shocked.

This was also the effect of the so-called ‘coordinating training’.

In short, he was going to let the disobedient people know who the real boss was and listen to his command obediently, in case they hurt themselves and others when they entered the relics.

In terms of combat ability, Li Linghai, who had just recovered to the Divinity Transformation Stage, was naturally no match for Ding Lingdang, who was in the prime of her life.

But now that he had returned to the peak from the dark abyss, his abundant experience and unwavering determination allowed him to deal with Ding Lingdang until Li Jialing took a breath and lunged at him again!

Chapter 2993: Targeted!

The four experts triggered their respective fields at the same time. Like four super-massive and dense planets, they formed their own gravity rings and magnetic fields and absorbed all the cosmic scraps nearby, igniting the Heavenly Fire of Prairie Fire in the high-speed friction.

Under the burning flames of spiritual energy, the shadow that was less than two meters tall had expanded to dozens of meters or even hundreds of meters tall. It was baring its fangs and brandishing its claws.

Ding Lingdang had really turned into a furious tyrannosaur. She stomped on the rings, tore apart the stars, and roared at the universe. Yan Liren, on the other hand, turned into a shadow of a sword and split into hundreds, thousands, and tens of thousands of sword nets that eclipsed the entire ring.

Li Linghai, on the other hand, turned into a nine-headed purple dragon that was wagging its tail, like a giant sea demon that had jumped out of an abyss.

Needless to say about Li Jialing, the golden lion had a pair of wings on its ribs, which had been fully grown and was roaring earsplittingly.

The four experts were engaged in a fierce battle. For a moment, sand and stones were flying everywhere. The sun and the moon were eclipsed. Ripples spread out in the vacuum unbelievably like surging tides.

In terms of combat ability, Li Jialing was no match for Ding Lingdang, and neither was Li Linghai. However, the territory of the emperor and the empress had a shocking resonance. The cooperation of the nine-headed purple dragon and the golden lion was flawless, and the territory was gradually melding into one. It was like a fire that was burning more furiously than ever.

Ding Lingdang and Yan Liren, on the other hand, were the kind of people who liked to fight alone. When they were engaged in a fierce battle, they did not even care about their companions.

Honestly speaking, even Ding Lingdang’s cooperation with Li Yao wasn’t that exquisite. She felt that Li Yao standing next to her was getting in the way and would hinder her from performing to her heart’s content, let alone Yan Liren.

The domains of the two teammates on the same side, on the other hand, were incompatible. They were like a cluster of frost, a cluster of flames. They touched, interfered, and blocked each other, melting each other with hissing noises. They were caught unprepared by the seamless cooperation of Li Linghai and Li Jialing.

BOOM!

Li Yao had gone through a long and deep mental training in the illusions created by the thousands of primeval crystal processors. In the boundless world of mortals, he had polished every side of his soul and created thousands of different personalities. He had a deeper understanding about the weakness of human nature, too, which allowed him to capture the emotions, desires, desires, and weaknesses of every expert. He chose the form that could cut into the enemy’s soul the most and created an illusion that could drown them without them knowing.

No. Other than Yan Liren, Xiao Ming, and Wen Wen, there was someone else.

It was Long Yangjun.

Long Yangjun was sitting cross-legged on a meteoroid that was as fast as lightning. She tilted her head and held her cheek, looking at the aggressive Li Yao in front of her suspiciously. She seemed to be deep in thought. Suddenly, she shook her head and mumbled to herself, “No.”

“What’s wrong?”

Li Yao grinned hideously. “Don’t you want to beat me up? Let’s fight!”

“This is very wrong.”

Long Yangjun said, “With your personality, there’s no reason for you to stay where you are and wait to be attacked by hundreds of experts. I thought you were going to play the same old trick and run away. I didn’t expect you to be so tough! No. I don’t believe it. This is not your style. No. No. Very wrong! “Although it looks the same as just now, I still feel that something is not right. Why did you divide everyone with raging flames so that we can’t perceive each other? Is it because your computational ability is insufficient and you can only simulate vivid environments, but you can’t simulate the reactions of hundreds of supreme experts?” “Got it. Another annoying illusion. Same move as Lu Qingchen.”

Long Yangjun pursed his lips. Before Li Yao, who was sitting in front of him, could retort, a translucent brilliance flashed in his eyes. “Hu—”

She blew at the vacuum of the universe.

If it were a vacuum, the air shouldn’t have been of any use.

But in this place, a ripple that looked like a crystal was expanding rapidly and corroding everything that could be corroded, including the meteoroids, the dust, the vacuum, and even Li Yao on the other side.

The entire world turned into a glittering world of crystals.

Long Yangjun snapped his fingers. The clusters of crystals collapsed and vanished. They reappeared in the planetary ring of the real world.

The illusion that Li Yao had painstakingly created was cracked by Long Yangjun so easily.

Long Yangjun gazed into the distance with a faint smile on his lips.

As it turned out, the burning Flaming Vulture and the fireball that separated all the top experts were gone-or rather, they had never existed in the first place.

In the middle of the rushing meteoroids, almost a hundred experts were dwelling in the illusions, unable to free themselves. Some of them were dancing crazily and fighting their imaginary enemies, some were thinking hard and trying to find a way to crack the illusionary labyrinth, and some even mistook other people for Li Yao.

The real Li Yao, on the other hand, was still crouching in the hollow of a gargantuan meteoroid, with a lot of weird-looking magical equipment floating around him. They were connected to a glittering metal hat on his head with thick crystal cables. Countless other devices that looked like loudspeakers and antennas were releasing spiritual energy nonstop.

“Are you serious?”

Long Yangjun’s frown grew deeper and deeper. “It’s just a friendly match, and you are even using magical equipment such as ‘brainwave amplifiers’. Do you have to be so… serious?”

At this point, the tip of her nose twitched as she sensed something. After a long while, she frowned to the extreme.

“I also sensed the remaining ripples after the crystal bombs exploded. It can’t be wrong. It is indeed one of your favorite formulas for crystal bombs. So, you didn’t blow up the meteoroids within a hundred square kilometers with your overwhelming spiritual pressure but planted a lot of crystal bombs in this area in advance?”

Long Yangjun spoke to himself and analyzed seriously, “Why are you doing this? I understand. You are intentionally creating a grand and dazzling sound and lightning effect to attract everyone’s attention and interfere with everyone’s perception so that your mental power can creep into everyone’s brain and launch a mental attack.

“Wait. Also, you’ve planted a lot of magical equipment such as ‘brainwave amplifiers’ and ‘mental power amplifiers’ in the dozens of square kilometers nearby. It’s like you’ve established your own local area network to improve your ability to build illusions. Wow. Li Yao, I’m not criticizing you, but is it necessary?” “You saw through me?”

Li Yao laughed in embarrassment. Then he said confidently, “Of course, it is necessary. This is the first time that I’ve shown up in front of all the heroes in the world as a ‘Soul Division Cultivator’. I’m definitely going to be the target of everyone’s attacks. If I fail to defeat everyone, I’m going to be ganged up on. Wouldn’t that be a disgrace? That’s why I played some tricks. Preparation is part of combat, too. We are not really muscular, simple-minded warriors!”

“Alright.”

Long Yangjun sighed and admitted, “At the very least, the fact that you are willing to resort to such sordid tricks is already much better than the illusion that you ran away with your tail between your legs under the cover of sound and electricity. “How is that possible? This is a crucial exploration operation that concerns the future of the civilization of mankind. Don’t be fooled by my naivety. When have I ever failed such an important operation?”

Li Yao said solemnly, “I did it in order to boast a little bit about my capability and intimidate the disobedient experts so that they would listen to orders and commands during the exploration operation. They should never act recklessly. Without the assistance of a large-scale magical equipment array, how could I trap hundreds of experts in illusions at the same time even if I drained all my brain cells?

“Hey, I am the best expert of the Pangu Universe after all. For such a noble purpose, and for your old friend, please do me a favor and cover up for me!” While they were talking, Li Yao had stored all the scraps—the ‘brainwave amplifiers’, the ‘mental power amplifiers’, etc. into his Cosmos Ring and blew up the meteorite below his feet, destroying all the evidence.

Then, a thunderous noise echoed from Li Yao’s chest to his throat.

The explosion echoed inside the heads of almost a hundred intoxicated experts at the same time, startling them out of their trance.

“Ah!”

The experts looked at each other in bewilderment. It was not until a long time later that they realized what was going on. Their internal organs constricted uncontrollably, and they felt cold all over their body. Their spine was freezing as if it had been soaked in cold water.

“It’s a mental attack! We are all trapped in an illusion!”

What a terrifying illusion-making ability. Infiltrating the brains of almost a hundred Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators and Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators at the same time and releasing serious interference and interference. Is this the horror of the Divinity Transformation Stage?

“He—he didn’t use his full strength at all, did he? He didn’t make any preparations or perform any techniques. He simply watched us casually and made us fall into his trap without us knowing? It was too terrifying, too awesome, and too unbelievable!”

“Soul Division. The legendary Soul Division is indeed immeasurable and unstoppable!”

The experts, especially the Immortal Cultivators of the Star Ocean Imperium who had been suspicious about Li Yao’s Divinity Transformation Stage, looked at Li Yao in awe and fear.

However, they were greatly surprised by Li Yao’s glittering appearance, which made him look like a devil!

Chapter 2997: The Path Of Illusion Breaking!

All the scraps of the stars nearby were attracted by Li Yao’s vitality. They were even dyed into colorful rainbows by him. They interweaved and tangled with each other like colorful streams and formed a magnificent lotus platform below Li Yao’s feet. Li Yao’s face was solemn, too. He looked more like a sage than Master Bitter Cicada, who had come from the ancient sacred world.

Li Yao sat cross-legged on the lotus platform and looked at everyone with a smile. There was indescribable tranquility and mystery in his eyes. All the experts knew that the expert in the Soul Division Stage was going to share his experience in training with them. It was an opportunity that could not be bought with money. They held their breath and listened attentively, not even daring to drop a drop of sweat.

Long Yangjun was the only one who held his forehead. He thought for a moment and was rendered speechless.

“In fact, as you all know, after you reach the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage, you will almost reach the limits of the strength and speed of the human body. Therefore, in regard to combat ability, there is no unbridgeable gap between the peak of the Nascent Soul Stage and the Divinity Transformation Stage.

Li Yao’s voice was transmitted to the auditory nerves of every expert with the help of invisible waves. “The limits we are talking about here are not just the limits of human beings but also the limits of the Pangu Clan, the Nuwa Clan, and even all the carbon-based intelligent creatures.

“The reason is simple. Although the growing environment of carbon-based intelligent life and the origin of civilization are different, every element that constitutes our flesh and blood-carbon, iron, oxygen, and so on-are of the same nature.

“It is decided then that carbon-based intelligent life is unlikely to be born on planets whose environments are too harsh and whose parameters are too extreme, such as cold planets that are too far away from the stars and live in -100°C year-round, desolate planets that have no atmosphere and are plagued by meteoroids year-round, or unstable planets where the stars have entered the senile stage and are ready for supernovas at any moment.

“The socialized, intelligent carbon-based creatures that can create a civilization can only be born on the planets that are similar to us in terms of standard gravity, standard atmosphere, and standard air. Even if there are some exceptions, such as the scorching planets where the Houyi Clan was born, the difference will not be huge.

SOI

“It is decided then that our limits are their limits, the limits of the Pangu Civilization and all the carbon-based intelligent creatures in the Pangu Universe, the limits of Pangu, Nuwa, Houyi, Kuafu, Gonggong, Zhurong, and so on.

“Moreover, I dare to say that, although the prehistoric species may boast techniques beyond the grasp of human beings in certain fields, considering all the fields from the perspective of universality and energy consumption ratio, human beings have evolved into the most perfect form of carbon-based intelligent life. Not only are we perfect in the Pangu Universe, we are perfect even in the multiverse. It is also the reason why the carbon-based intelligent life in the magical universe adopted the ‘human form’ as their fundamental form.

“What I’m trying to say is that I hope that you will not underestimate yourselves or be intimidated by the fame of the Pangu Clan. I can promise you solemnly that, even if the Pangu Clan inside the ancient tomb is resurrected, their combat ability will not be much higher than ours, at least not to the extent of a world of difference.

Li Yao’s declaration was so resolute that it seemed to have blown the horn of glory in everyone’s heart.

No one had expected that he would say such a thing. But when they realized that it was the final conclusion of the division of the strongest Cultivators, they were more or less convinced and emboldened.

“But-“

Li Yao changed the topic and said solemnly, “After we break into the Divinity Transformation Stage or even the Dividing Soul Stage, the importance of the physical body and the material world will gradually diminish. The importance of the field of will and the mental power will gradually increase. Or rather, the intelligent creatures who call themselves the best of all creatures must learn to see through the truth that binds our body of flesh and blood and even the material world to pursue the truth of a higher level.

“You all think that I cast an illusion just now and dragged you into an illusion space, but can you really tell what is an illusion, what is a virtual illusion space, and what is the real universe?

“You think that you are living in the real universe and that every cell that constitutes your body is real. It is perfectly justified and unquestionable. Such an understanding gives you a great sense of security, but it is also a barrier that limits your potential.

“Cells, what are cells, molecules, molecules, atoms, and atoms? What is there to divide atoms other than energy? Nothing! Then, here comes the question. Since the tiniest atoms that make up our bodies are transformed from energy, why can’t the illusionary space that I created by interfering with your vision, hearing, and thinking cells with my mental power be a reality?

“Of course, I haven’t been in the Soul Division for long, and I don’t have the ability to maintain the illusionary space inside your brain for a long time. But if there is someone thousands of times stronger than me who can extend the duration of the mental attack to the point that you will be trapped in the illusionary space for the rest of your life, is such an attack an illusion or reality?”

Everybody was confused by Li Yao’s mysterious words and did not understand what he meant.

“Please consider the question carefully. What I’m trying to say is that dozens of Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivators and almost a hundred Nascent Soul Stage Cultivators with Giant God Soldiers are participating in our operation to explore the Mausoleum of the Ancients. It is the largest adventure team in the history of human civilization with the highest average combat ability. I’m not worried that you will encounter material attacks. Even if you do, no one will be able to stop us as long as we are united!

Li Yao said, “However, it’s hard to say when it comes to mental attacks. After all, our team is not very cohesive. There are quite a few gaps between us and we have our own plans.

“Be careful! Be very careful! Whether it is Lu Qingchen, the Pangu Clan, or the Flood Tide Legion, it is very likely that they are all weird existences who are adept at mental attacks. One moment of carelessness, and you will fall into their trap. You will be swallowed by your own desires and the weakness of your humanity, and you will become their pawns and puppets!

“In the Cultivation system of the ancient Cultivation world, the last barrier before the Cultivation of True Fruit is known as the ‘Void Enlightenment Stage’. I believe that the Void Enlightenment Stage is the stage where one can distinguish the truth and falsehood of everything around them. One can even distinguish the truth and falsehood of one’s own desires, get rid of the unnecessary illusions, and cultivate the most authentic side of oneself. Only in such a way can one be free from the temptations and troubles of the outside world. One can be as immovable as a mountain and persist in moving forward.

“It is like a chess piece on a chess board. If you are obsessed with the chess board and train yourself to be stronger at any cost, you will only be the ‘strongest chess piece’ in the end. You will be bound by the rules of the chess game. Only by knowing the truth about yourself and jumping out of the chess board can you see, understand, and cultivate to a higher level, a higher dimension, and a more mysterious and boundless power!

“The road of evolution has come to an end. There is no road ahead.

“Let’s hope that we can find the wings of the soul and soar beyond the limits of evolution in the adventure of the Mausoleum of Ancient Gods!”

On the third day after the training, all the preparations had been completed.

The experts who had been injured and exhausted during the training had recovered to their peak state with the nourishment of Heavenly Materials and Earthly Treasures and strengthening drugs.

Their crystal suits and Giant Soldiers had undergone the final modification and security checks, too. On average, each of them was carrying three to four crystal suits, as well as countless crystals and ammunitions. They were all wearing Cosmos Rings that were more than enough for a space jump. The names of the assistants from various universities and research centers had been carefully selected, too. In the end, they were all the top experts in archeology, history, biology, runology, medicine, and so on. Many of them were old men with white hair and a ruddy complexion. They had high prestige in the circle and had disciples all over the world.

For such authoritative experts, it was naturally impossible to force them to take part in the risky adventure. Therefore, they were only invited to the Seven Seas Space Zone as ‘advisers’ for a period of closed training and guidance. It was not until the last moment that the truth of the ‘Emperor Tomb-God’s Tomb

– Black Tomb’ was revealed to them. They were told that the adventure was extremely dangerous and that they would not be forced to join it if they were unwilling. However, due to confidentiality issues, they had to stay in the training base of the Seven Seas Space Zone for a while longer and could not return to their hometown until the adventure team returned.

As he expected, almost no authoritative expert was willing to give up such a great opportunity.

Ancient Tomb of the Supreme Emperor! Ancient Tomb! Black Tomb!

The Supreme Emperor! The Pangu Clan! The maker of the black wall!

For any expert, such an unprecedented secret was right in front of their eyes. It was indeed something worth exploring and studying at the risk of their own life.

The construction of the team of specialists went very smoothly. The selection and establishment of the coalition fleet was not a problem, either. Considering the complicated environment near the ancient tomb, which was as harsh as a space hell, it was impossible to summon a large-scale expedition army. In the end, a special squad made of almost a hundred fast attack starships, seven comprehensive supply starships, and two research starships was formed.

All the starships had been further strengthened to ensure their anti-radiation, anti-high-energy particle streams, anti-strong magnetic field interference, and anti-meteoroid attacks.

In the meantime, Li Linghai launched a lot of new probe magical equipment to his destination and collected countless local astronomical parameters to infer the changes of the environment near the entrance of the ancient tomb.

The situation was not optimistic.

Chapter 2998: Terrifying Ancient Superstar

If the twin nebula where the entrance of the ancient tomb was located was an overwhelming, splendid butterfly, the left wing of the two butterflies would be known as ‘Twin One’, while the right wing would be known as ‘Twin Two’.

Of the two galaxies, the one with the largest size and the most mass was the fifth planet, which was 1,724 times the diameter of Empyrean Terminus and 445 times the mass of Empyrean Terminus. It was like a giant among planets and close to the limit of the planetary stability structure. It was an out-and-out ‘giant planet’.

Since the Gemini Nebula was very far away from the Star Ocean Imperium, even if the astronomers of the past had observed the spectacular scene of the supernova outbreak, they had not done much research on the chaotic space zone, nor had they named all the planets that were enveloped by the Gemini Nebula. Therefore, in the original astronomical database of the Star Ocean Imperium, the giant planet was simply known as ‘Twin Left Five’, which meant the fifth planet of the Gemini No. 1 galaxy.

But right now, the cosmologists were paying much more attention to the planet, because the entrance to the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was probably in the orbit of the planet. In other words, the entrance to the tomb of the Supreme Emperor had been captured by the gravity of the planet and seemed to have become one of its ‘satellites’.

Therefore, the cosmologists named it ‘Giant Star’.

Ancient giants were very typical planets.

The giant of the family of planets was a gas-liquid planet that was mainly made of hydrogen and helium and occupied more than 50% of its total mass. The cosmologists suspected that a solid core made of various heavy elements was inside the planet, but the time was too short for detailed exploration. They could only infer from the data of other planets.

According to the deduction of ordinary planets, the outer layer of such planets was often made of carbon, ethane, hydrogen sulfide, mercury, and frozen crystals. If abundant resources of crystals were stored inside the planets, super-intensity radiation and high-energy particle streams would be released under the stimulation of the stars and the enormous internal stresses. Generally speaking, such planets were the reserves of the stars, or ‘quasi-stellar stars’ that could not be turned into stars without reaching the critical point. The energy that a planet could release to the outside world was even greater than the energy it absorbed from the stars.

The energy, together with the high rotation speed, turned the outer layer of the planet into a chaotic, furious ocean of energy. It was as if the entire planet was enveloped in an ocean of lightning and thunder. Even the most dense and solid meteoroids would be torn apart by the furious teeth of the planet if they tried to invade its territory recklessly.

A planet in the stable period of a galaxy was already so terrifying.

In the meantime, the supernova outbreak of the Gemini Nebula had shattered the stability of the two galaxies. When the two stars burst out, they would spurt out overloaded energy and mass. When they hit the stability of the ‘ancient giant’, they would break the shackles that the stars imposed on them and make them look like wild horses that had run out of their reins or ambitious barbarians when they were leaderless.

The reason was simple.

It was by relying on a gravitational field hundreds of times more than its mass that the disobedient planet was captured and bound to its orbit.

But right now, due to the loss of mass and energy in the supernova outbreak, the newly-born neutron stars were no longer able to drag the ‘ancient giant star’.

When a supernova broke out, the blast of tremendous energy and mass broke through the outer layer of the ‘ancient giant’ and the middle layer of the liquid state. It was very likely that it would invade the core of the solid state and trigger an immeasurable, unbelievable reaction with the heavy elements inside, triggering the crazy evolution.

On one hand, since the ancient giants were no longer bound by the stars, their orbits had become twisted and irregular. Maybe after a ‘short moment’ in the scale of the universe, they would be freed from the seals of the Gemini Nebula and fly to the edge of the universe, turning into a wandering planet.

On the other hand, all the materials that constituted the ancient giants were being compressed and heated by gravity, trying to raise the core temperature to more than ten million degrees in order to trigger a fusion reaction and replace the two aging neutron stars as new stars!

Ancient giants were the most massive celestial bodies in the two galaxies other than stars.

It was like the strongest child in the family was always yearning to replace the father.

Once it successfully awakened the Heart of the Star and ignited a raging fire of millions of degrees, it would be possible to engulf the entire Gemini Nebula and create a new, stable stellar system.

But it was a pity that its experiment was going to fail, because its mass was still too small to reach the threshold of the birth of a star.

Another important reason was that the supernova outbreaks of the two stars had spurted out a lot of materials and energy. At the intersection, another experiment of ‘star birth’ was taking away 50% of its hope. The only outcome would be ‘mutual destruction’. Neither of the two celestial bodies would turn into stars and return to silence. However, before they died down, their struggles and efforts would release the most furious radiation, ripples, high-energy particle streams, and strong magnetic fields. For stars and planets, it was just a blink of an eye or a sneeze. But for human beings and starships, it was a catastrophe. Even for an expert in the division of soul such as Li Yao, it was still unknown whether or not his vitality could be fully utilized in the most intense magnetic field.

Other than that, like most planets, the ancient giants boasted almost a hundred satellites.

As the entire galaxy and their home planets mutated, the stable orbits of the satellites were shattered, too. Some satellites were attracted by the attraction force of the ancient stars and dived into the embrace of the main star, triggering furious torrents of energy and mass hundreds of thousands of meters high that looked like volcanic eruptions. The trajectories of some satellites overlapped and crashed into each other violently. They were shattered into pieces and formed random, unpredictable stone belts that swallowed everything on their way with a destructive aura.

Also, many satellites had been hiding a lot of highly radioactive elements in the first place, which formed a furious magnetic field that could even disable all the functions of the starships that entered it.

That would be terrible.

It must be noted that the ancient giants were in an extremely unstable state. The density and mass of their body parts were different. Their gravity was different, too. They were like gravity traps or ‘chaotic sources’. When the starship of the adventure team had 100% power, they were naturally cautious enough to keep a safe distance from the ancient star. However, if the power unit of the starship was blown up by a meteoroid, or the navigation system was jammed by a magnetic storm, the starship would fall into the orbit of the ancient star and be faced with endless lightning

It was the worst situation that a starship could ever encounter.

Even if a Divinity Transformation Stage Cultivator was riding a Giant God Soldier, they might not be able to escape from the lightning, thunder, and magnetic storms hundreds of times the standard gravity.

If they were captured by the gravity and fell into the depths of the ancient giants, they would only be crushed into solid bodies made of flesh and metal.

This was how terrifying the ancient giants

were.

The deeper he went, the more unexpected dangers he would encounter. They were ten times more dangerous than the problems he knew.

However, it couldn’t be helped. The entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor was in the orbit of the ancient star. Only a behemoth such as the ancient star could attract it and make it move in a way that was somewhat predictable and could be calculated.

It was now or never. If the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor got rid of the gravity of the ancient giants and moved irregularly in the middle of the vast, boundless Twin Stars Nebula, nobody would be able to find it.

“Lu Qingchen is truly a lunatic!”

Looking at the latest astronomical parameters and environmental monitoring files, Li Yao frowned hard. “We have grasped so many terrifying parameters and made thorough preparations, but we did not dare to rush into such a place recklessly. Lu Qingchen dared to break into such a ‘forbidden area of life’ with only five supreme fortresses? He must’ve made up his mind to die or die trying!

“But then again, we are truly unlucky. The sea of stars is so vast, but the entrance to the tomb of the Supreme Emperor has to be in such a chaotic, brutal place?

Li Linghai, on the other hand, told Li Yao that it was not a matter of luck, and that he was being unreasonable.

The Tomb of the Supreme Emperor was not in a stable three-dimensional space. Of course, it was not in the legendary four-dimensional space, either. It could be said that it was in a weird space fragment in the folds of the three-dimensional universe.

If the three-dimensional universe remained stable without the slightest gap, the tomb of the Supreme Emperor naturally wouldn’t appear.

Only in the extremely harsh environments such as the Gemini Nebula and the ancient giants, where all the materials and energy in the entire space were undergoing the most violent changes, could the stable structure of the three-dimensional space be torn apart in the super high-speed collisions between planets and magnetic fields, thereby attracting the entrance of the tomb of the Supreme Emperor from which it would ‘surface’.

In the past, the entrance to the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor had always appeared in such a chaotic astronomical environment. When Li Linghai entered the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor for the first time, he had also encountered a soul-stirring cosmic storm and ended up in it by accident. Later, when the Tomb of the Supreme Emperor was being developed, he had encountered all kinds of dangers, too. However, the astronomical environment this time was particularly harsh. But it also proved that the diameter of the space gap was long enough and the maintenance time was long enough. Therefore, a large batch of experts and hundreds of starships were able to enter the tomb and carry out the largest scale of development!

All in all, risks and benefits coexisted. Whether they were faced with Lu Qingchen, the Pangu Clan, the Flood Tide Legion, or the furious universe itself, the only thing that mattered to them was the victory of the brave!

Chapter 2999: Deep Into The Butterfly

“Space jump, ten seconds to go.”

Li Yao didn’t know how many times he had heard such a cold and calm voice, but he was still as excited as when he first performed a space jump.

Even though he had reached the Divinity Transformation Stage, he still felt insignificant when he was faced with the vast, unpredictable universe!

“Countdown. Five seconds to go. Five, four, three…”

The almost transparent buffer gel protected every inch of his body so that he would not be hurt by the space-time ripples. Then again, if he was really engaged in a chaotic storm in the four-dimensional space, the buffer gel would be of no help even if it was ten times thicker. Such a buffer gel would only relieve his discomfort after the safe jump and bring him some illusionary comfort.

The universe was never a gentle shelter. The warriors who dared to challenge it were destined to die.

With such considerations in mind, Li Yao, Ding Lingdang, and all the other experts boarded different starships. Almost every starship had a complete exploration squad made of commanders, firepower experts, combat experts, doctors, and archeologists who could work independently.

In such a way, even if 90% of the starships were wrecked in the cosmic storm, there was still hope for the mission to be completed.

“Two, one… launch!”

The calm countdown suddenly turned high-pitched. Li Yao felt that somebody had kicked his soft brain brutally from the inside. Then, the deck and the cabins around him turned transparent, before they turned into stars again. The stars rushed forward at a high speed, leaving helical lines behind them. He seemed to have been spun thousands of times in one second and shot out brutally.

The feeling lasted for a minute or a million years.

He tried his best to release his telepathic thoughts in order to stabilize his soul in the four-dimensional space and even explore the mysteries of the four-dimensional space, but to no avail. He could not focus his attention at all. All he could see was one or a million shining stars. Before he could count the number of stars, the brain matter that had been tossed far away crashed back into his skull. His internal organs, nerves, veins, and every cell returned to their place. He and the starship returned to the three-dimensional universe again and landed neatly.

“This is…”

Li Yao immediately connected to the mainframe crystal processor of the starship and the tactical network of the entire exploration fleet and determined his location through the scanning of almost a hundred starships.

They were only 0.1 lightyears away from the Gemini Nebula.

It was a marvelous long-distance jump. Judging from the vastness of the sea of stars, they reached the edge of the Gemini Nebula almost instantly. The distance of less than 0.1 lightyears was within the range of normal sailing. If they had enough time and patience, they could activate the power units and fly there slowly with the help of the crystal fuel. The good thing was that it was safe enough. They could also scan and analyze the astronomical environment of the Gemini Nebula. “So… beautiful!”

Looking at the view of the Gemini Nebula through the virtual window, Li Yao was somewhat dazed.

The supernova explosion of two neighboring stars almost took place at the same time, leaving a deep, colorful trace in the darkness of the universe. It was truly like an overwhelming butterfly that was stretching its splendid wings. The endless colors and helixes on the wings were never seen in the deepest nightmares of the craziest artists. Looking at it, Li Yao even had a weird feeling that it seemed to be alive—not a life defined by the intelligent carbon-based creatures, but a life of a higher level.

Who said it was impossible?

Who said that stars, planets, and nebulae could not be the same life form, and carbon-based intelligent life forms were just fleas and specks of dust on the bodies of such cosmic life forms?

But even if they were just fleas and dust, since they existed for one day, they had to explore the meaning of their existence and see clearly how magnificent and beautiful the world they were in was!

“Stainless Steel Mouse has arrived at the designated coordinates safely!”
